NVIDIA V2D Challenge · Track 2 Robotic Grounding · Unitree G1 + Dex3

V2D Track 2 工作文档

截至 10/2 07:00（UTC-6）。Tier 2 第 1 批（27 条逐帧满配：30 条去掉 ep1、ep7、ep28 三条方盒支撑面的序列，参考是 v5.4 净室降噪的结果）A、B 段两台都已训完，26 条有最终 policy，都没有非有限参数；ep10 white_desk_lower_to_ground_10 排在 ml40 的 C 段，还没开始。按批量判据对照 Tier 2 v5.4 参考：A 段 9 条通过 1 条（mf4090 的 ep6，边缘），mf4090 的 B 段 8 条都不通过，共同原因是 MP-SR 为 0；ml40 的 B 段 07:00 开始评测。这些分数不和 Tier 1 比。8c778f9 兼容性验证的 mf4090 部分 06:43 通过，7 行全部符合：v0.4.0 训出的 checkpoint 能在 8c778f9 里加载、导出，同一 checkpoint 在两个评测器下 4096 世界的 ADD-AUC 差 0.0014。方盒重训随即在 mf4090 开始，4 条冒烟都通过；ml40 的兼容性验证排在 C 段之后。用户 10/1 23:50 定下工作顺序：先做完提交流程与两档各 30 条导出，再诊断 MP-SR 为 0 的序列，最后用一个改动后的配方小规模试验；并决定暂不往 Kaggle 上传任何内容。提交流程计划第 2 版已审、已部署，两台的导出链在等方盒评测结束，预计 16:30–17:30 导完。见 Tier 2 第 1 批、官方评测与提交。

Tier 1（截至 9/30 14:40）：第 1 批 21 条逐帧满配全部结束，按批量判定脚本的判据通过 9 条、不通过 9 条、无效 3 条（ep1、ep7、ep28）。连同之前的 9 条，30 条里 27 条有最终 checkpoint 的判定，MP-SR ≥ 0.95 的 5 条、0.5–0.95 的 2 条、0 到 0.5 之间 6 条、恰好为 0 的 14 条。无效的三条都用方盒支撑面：FlashCHORD v0.4.0 的导入器不读 USD 的缩放，把方盒建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体，参考物体在第 0 帧就已嵌进立方体（未核实）。上游 #185 已修。ml40 上的 GPU 对照 14:35 全部跑完，按登记判据 A.3 的最终判定是 H1′ 支持：ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）配对种子 43–50，v0.4.0 的导入器 5 / 8 发散，换上 #185 0 / 8，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128。能下的结论是 v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发，ep1 只有单种子的旁证、方向相同，ep28 只描述；fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少，这组对照回答不了。组委会 10/1 13:43 重新发布 kit，评测运行时改钉在 8c778f9，这个版本已含 #185。原定要问组委会的前三问因此由我们自行定下（决定记录 agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md，jev 9 / 9 核实）：评测与导出用 8c778f9；方盒三条两档各重训一条；只有地面的序列继续用 scene.support=false。要能提交，还差方盒 6 条的 policy（在训）、两档各 30 条的导出与打包（导出链已起，等方盒评测结束），以及 Kaggle 上两件要用户做的事：接受 tier2-auc 的规则，把各比赛的队伍名改成 maniphys.ai。封榜 2026-11-04 17:00 EST，从 10/2 算还有 33 天。

封榜时间待确认

未合并的规则草案（PR #164）里另有 11/5 和 11/10 两个日期。

1 / 17Tier 2 已判定的 17 条（A 段 9 条、mf4090 的 B 段 8 条）里按批量判据通过的（mf4090 的 ep6，边缘）；对照的是 Tier 2 v5.4 参考，不和 Tier 1 比
7 / 7mf4090 上 8c778f9 兼容性验证符合的行数：4 份导出的加载、结构与来历都过，同一 checkpoint 在两个评测器下 4096 世界的 ADD-AUC 差 0.0014；ml40 排在 C 段之后
27 / 30Tier 1 有最终判定的逐帧满配，按批量判据通过 11 条；剩下 ep1、ep7、ep28 的方盒支撑面在 v0.4.0 里是 1 m 立方体，按登记判据是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发（p = 0.0128）。新 kit 钉的 8c778f9 已含 #185，这三条两档各重训一条：mf4090 的 4 条 10/2 06:43 开训，ml40 的 2 条排在 C 段之后
5 / 27MP-SR ≥ 0.95：ep12 0.992、ep8 0.979、ep23 0.975、ep24 0.962、ep26 0.956
14 / 27MP-SR 恰好为 0；路程、总转角对 MP-SR 的秩相关 −0.69、−0.65（事后描述）
33 天从 10/2 到封榜（11/4，待确认）

“通过”指批量判定脚本 stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py 的判据（chord_sr > 0、MP-SR > 0、ADD-AUC 高于这条序列的不动基线），不是排行榜上的成功率。MP-SR 是本地 4096 个世界里成功的比例，排行榜每条 episode 只评 1 个世界。来源：results/day2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_batch1{a,b}.txt、results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt、results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt、results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt、results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt、results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md（“最终判定”一节）、results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt。运行时与三问的决定：agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md、results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt、results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt。Tier 2 第 1 批：results/t2/status_{ml40,mf4090}_0700.txt、results/t2/pace_{ml40,mf4090}_t2b_{2000,0700}.txt、results/t2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_t2a.txt、results/t2/batch_verdict_mf4090_t2b.txt、results/t2/ep10_smoke_failure_ml40.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-01-t2-batch.md。兼容性验证与方盒：results/submission/compat/mf4090_*.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md。提交流程：agent-doc/plan-submission-exports-2026-10-02.md、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-submission-exports.md。Kaggle：results/submission/kaggle_join_status_1002_0700.txt。

NVIDIA V2D Challenge · Track 2 Robotic Grounding · Unitree G1 + Dex3

V2D Track 2 working notes

As of 07:00 on 10/2 (UTC-6). Tier 2 batch 1 (27 frame-playback full-recipe runs: the 30 episodes minus the three box-support sequences ep1, ep7 and ep28, trained on the v5.4 clean-room denoised references) has finished training segments A and B on both machines: 26 runs have a final policy, none with a non-finite parameter; ep10 white_desk_lower_to_ground_10 waits in segment C on ml40, which has not started. Under the batch criteria, against the Tier 2 v5.4 references, 1 of the 9 segment A runs passes (ep6 on mf4090, marginally) and all 8 of mf4090's segment B runs fail, all with MP-SR 0; ml40 started evaluating segment B at 07:00. These scores are not compared with Tier 1. The mf4090 half of the 8c778f9 compatibility check passed at 06:43 with all 7 rows as expected: checkpoints trained with v0.4.0 load and export in 8c778f9, and the same checkpoint scores an ADD-AUC within 0.0014 under the two evaluators on 4096 worlds. The box retrains started on mf4090 right after, and all 4 smokes passed; ml40's compatibility check follows segment C. On 10/1 at 23:50 the user set the order of work: first the submission flow and the exports of all 30 episodes per tier, then a diagnosis of the sequences with MP-SR 0, then a small trial of one modified recipe; and decided not to upload anything to Kaggle for now. The submission flow plan v2 has been reviewed and deployed, and the export chains on both machines wait for the box evaluations, expected to finish exporting between 16:30 and 17:30. See Tier 2 batch 1 and Official evaluation and submission.

Tier 1 (as of 14:40 on 9/30): all 21 frame-playback full-recipe runs of batch 1 are done: by the batch verdict script's criteria, 9 pass, 9 fail and 3 are invalid (ep1, ep7, ep28). With the 9 earlier runs, 27 of the 30 episodes have a verdict on the final checkpoint. MP-SR is at least 0.95 for 5 of them, between 0.5 and 0.95 for 2, between 0 and 0.5 for 6, and exactly 0 for 14. The three invalid episodes all use box supports: the FlashCHORD v0.4.0 importer ignores the USD scale and builds each box as a solid cube with 1 m edges, and the reference object is already inside the cube at frame 0 (unverified). Upstream #185 fixes this. The GPU controls on ml40 all finished at 14:35, and the final verdict under the registered criteria A.3 is that H1′ is supported: in ep7 smokes (4M steps, L40S) with paired seeds 43–50, 5 of 8 runs with the v0.4.0 importer diverge against 0 of 8 with #185, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128. What we can conclude is that v0.4.0 building the box as a 1 m cube triggers the divergence of the ep7 smokes; ep1 has only single-seed supporting evidence pointing the same way, and ep28 is description only. These controls cannot say how much fp16 storage and the BatchNorm statistics each contribute. The organizers republished the kit at 13:43 on 10/1 and now pin the evaluation runtime to 8c778f9, which includes #185. We therefore settled the first three questions we had meant to ask them ourselves (decision record agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md, 9 of 9 claims verified with jev): evaluate and export with 8c778f9; retrain the three box sequences once in each tier; keep scene.support=false for floor-only sequences. Before we can submit we still need the policies of the 6 box runs (training), the exports and packing of all 30 episodes per tier (the export chains are armed and wait for the box evaluations), and two Kaggle steps for the user: accept the tier2-auc rules and rename the team to maniphys.ai in each competition. The freeze is 2026-11-04 17:00 EST, 33 days from 10/2.

Freeze date unconfirmed

The unmerged draft rules (PR #164) also mention November 5 and November 10.

26 / 27Tier 2 batch 1 full runs finished by 07:00 on 10/2 (segments A and B), no final policy with a non-finite parameter; ep10 moved to segment C on ml40 after a W&B network timeout and has not started
1 / 17Tier 2 runs judged so far (9 in segment A, 8 in mf4090's segment B) that pass the batch criteria (ep6 on mf4090, marginally), measured against the Tier 2 v5.4 references and not compared with Tier 1
7 / 7rows of the 8c778f9 compatibility check on mf4090 that came out as expected: the 4 exports load with the right structure and provenance, and the same checkpoint scores an ADD-AUC within 0.0014 under the two evaluators on 4096 worlds; ml40 follows segment C
27 / 30Tier 1 episodes with a verdict on a final frame-playback full-recipe checkpoint, 11 passing the batch criteria; the box supports of the other three (ep1, ep7, ep28) are 1 m cubes in v0.4.0, which the registered criteria find to trigger the ep7 smoke divergence (p = 0.0128). 8c778f9, the runtime the new kit pins, includes #185; the three are being retrained once per tier: the 4 runs on mf4090 started at 06:43 on 10/2, and the 2 on ml40 follow segment C
5 / 27MP-SR ≥ 0.95: ep12 0.992, ep8 0.979, ep23 0.975, ep24 0.962, ep26 0.956
14 / 27MP-SR exactly 0; rank correlation of path length and total rotation with MP-SR −0.69 and −0.65 (post hoc description)
33 daysFrom 10/2 to the freeze (11/4, unconfirmed)

"Pass" means the criteria of the batch verdict script stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py (chord_sr > 0, MP-SR > 0, ADD-AUC above the sequence's do-nothing baseline), not a leaderboard success rate. MP-SR is the fraction of 4096 local worlds that succeed; the leaderboard evaluates one world per episode. Sources: results/day2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_batch1{a,b}.txt, results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt, results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt, results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt, results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt, results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt, agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md (the final verdict section), results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt. Runtime and the three decisions: agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md, results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt, results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt. Tier 2 batch 1: results/t2/status_{ml40,mf4090}_0700.txt, results/t2/pace_{ml40,mf4090}_t2b_{2000,0700}.txt, results/t2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_t2a.txt, results/t2/batch_verdict_mf4090_t2b.txt, results/t2/ep10_smoke_failure_ml40.txt, agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-01-t2-batch.md. Compatibility check and box runs: results/submission/compat/mf4090_*.txt, agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md. Submission flow: agent-doc/plan-submission-exports-2026-10-02.md, agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-submission-exports.md. Kaggle: results/submission/kaggle_join_status_1002_0700.txt.

Top three right now

  • Running
    Submission flow and exports of all 30 episodes per tier (step 1 of the order of work)Plan v2, agent-doc/plan-submission-exports-2026-10-02.md, was deployed early on 10/2 after the Fable 5.1 review (GO WITH FIXES, no blocker). The run used for each episode follows a registered rule, not the scores (stages/evaluate/exports/); ep20 uses the batch run t1-ep20-w2048-cap4m-mf4090, which the user can change. The Tier 2 frame references for packing were generated from v5.4, and all 30 pass the sanity checks. The export chains on both machines wait for the box evaluations: each episode is exported on one world with 8c778f9, then its structure, provenance and candidate condition (a clean 4096-world evaluation of the same checkpoint) are checked, expected to finish between 16:30 and 17:30; the Mac then packs the two CSVs with the official packer and runs the uploader with --dry-run only. On 10/1 the user decided not to upload anything to Kaggle for now (agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-submission-exports.md)
    rxzhou
  • Running
    The 6 box retrains and the 8c778f9 compatibility checkThe compatibility check on mf4090 passed at 06:43 (all 7 rows as expected), and the box chain started right after: all 4 smokes passed, and the full runs go t1-ep1, t1-ep7, t1-ep28, t2-ep28, expected to finish around 16:00. ml40's compatibility check and its 2 box runs (t2-ep1, t2-ep7) follow Tier 2 segment C. One world and 4096 worlds can differ a lot: exported on one world, t1-ep6 has MP-SR 0, while the same checkpoint reaches MP-SR 0.57 on 4096 worlds (agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md)
    rxzhou
  • Running
    Wrap up Tier 2 batch 1Segments A and B are trained on both machines. mf4090's 8 segment B runs are judged and all fail, all with MP-SR 0 (results/t2/batch_verdict_mf4090_t2b.txt); ml40 started evaluating segment B at 07:00, then runs segment C (ep10), ending around 10:15. The scores are measured against the Tier 2 v5.4 references; they are not compared with Tier 1 and not used to tune the method
    rxzhou

  • 现状各环节能不能用、卡在哪；Tier 2 第 1 批的进度与判定，8c778f9 兼容性验证，方盒重训与提交流程的进度7 个环节

Two submission rules: a policy with NaN is never exported; if an episode fails with must disable task failure termination, fall back to an earlier finite checkpoint of the same run. The new packer solves one rigid transform from frame 0 that maps the evaluator reference onto the tier's released input, and every frame must agree within 1 mm and 1e-3 rad; Tier 2 trains on the v5.4 references, so packing it needs --frame-references. Tier 2 降噪

Tier 2 的噪声是从 0 开始、有上限的随机游走（旋转与平移上限约 30° / 30 mm）。静止段里观测到的增量就是漂移增量，v1 以后的各版本都建立在这一点上。下图为各版本对 Tier 1 真值的 ADD-AUC。

读图

虚线框的 v5.2、v5.3 参数来自 Tier 1，合规有疑问，只作参照。绿色的 v5.4 用于提交，所有参数只由 Tier 2 推出，代价 −0.041，见 ADR-00002。

被数据否定的方向（4 条）
方向结果来源
纯时序平滑+0.001smooth_baseline.txt
用手锚定物体（完美根也不行）0.225oracle_root.txt
抓握刚性：干净数据上按手传播0.225，段末 50° / 136 mmgrasp_rigidity.txt
手的逐帧增量融合噪声是漂移步长的 2.1 倍increment_noise.txt

Tier 2 denoising

Tier 2 noise is a capped random walk that starts at zero (caps of about 30° and 30 mm). Inside a static segment, every observed increment is a drift increment, and every version from v1 on builds on that fact. The chart shows ADD-AUC of each version against the Tier 1 ground truth.

Reading the chart

The dashed bars, v5.2 and v5.3, use parameters derived from Tier 1, so their compliance is doubtful; they are shown for reference only. The green bar, v5.4, is the submission version: every parameter comes from Tier 2 alone, at a cost of −0.041. See ADR-00002.

Directions ruled out by data (4)
DirectionResultSource
Pure temporal smoothing+0.001smooth_baseline.txt
Anchoring the object to the hand (fails even with a perfect root)0.225oracle_root.txt
Grasp rigidity: propagating with the hand on clean data0.225, 50° / 136 mm at segment endgrasp_rigidity.txt
Fusing per-frame hand incrementsnoise is 2.1× the drift stepincrement_noise.txt

特殊情况

截至 9/30 14:40。分成序列与参考、评测与运行环境两张表；方盒支撑面那一行牵涉 ep1、ep7、ep28 三条，也牵涉提交。

序列与参考

情况处理
方盒支撑面在 v0.4.0 里被建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体（ep1 vacuum_left_lean_02 的椅背、ep7 iron_round_table_02 的桌面、ep28 squeegee_sweep_away_from_you_03 的两块椅背板）。v0.4.0 scene/support.py:85–86, 114–116 对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale，实际建出来半尺寸都是 (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) m（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）；kit 指定的 aee95f2 同样（results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt）。按这个几何，参考物体从第 0 帧起就在方块里：ep7 896 / 896 帧嵌入超过 5 mm，第 0 帧最深约 49 cm（这条 jev 未核实，数字直接取自脚本输出）；ep1 519 / 747 帧；ep28 两块分别 608、651 / 988 帧（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt）。第 1 批里这三条都没有结果：ep1 从 50,001,920 步起每份 checkpoint 都含 NaN、3 项评测报错，ep28 看守在 60,002,304 步停训，ep7 冒烟后跳过上游 8c778f905c（#185，9/29 12:11）已修，Cube 按 size 乘 xformOp:scale 建。ml40 上的补丁副本（V2D_FC_SRC，见现状）建出的 ep7 桌面半尺寸 (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) m（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt）。GPU 对照按登记判据的最终判定（14:35，results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt）：H1′ 支持。ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S、fp16 存储）用 v0.4.0 时 8 个种子里 5 条出 NaN，用 #185 时 0 / 8（配对种子 43–50），单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128；ep1 截到 6000 万步，v0.4.0 出 NaN、#185 全部有限（L40S、单种子，只作旁证）。能说的范围：v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发，ep1 的单种子旁证同向；ep28 登记为只描述；fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少说不了。10/1 的新 kit 把评测运行时钉在 8c778f9（含 #185），这三条按 #185 的几何两档各重训一条（方盒计划第 2 版已部署：mf4090 的 4 条 10/2 06:43 开训，ml40 的 2 条排在 Tier 2 C 段之后）。详见发散排查
ep28 squeegee_sweep_away_from_you_03 按 #185 的几何（板的标注尺寸）参考仍穿过两块 2 cm 板：239、209 帧有顶点从板的另一侧穿出（results/diverge/support_penetration.txt）登记为只描述、不用来判任何假设：#185 冒烟最终回合 87.0、回报 4.46；截到 6000 万步 6 份 checkpoint 全部有限，最终回合 295.8、回报 54.72（results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L2_ml40.txt）。重训前先看参考。mf4090 上 runs/flash_chord/t1-ep28-w2048-cap4m-mf4090 留着 50,001,920、60,002,304 步的 state（对应的 policy 都含非有限参数）、没有 DONE；STOP 标记在 fc_run 退出时已删掉（fc_run.sh:211–212）。直接用 fc_run.sh 重跑同名 run 会从 60,002,304 步那份 NaN state 续训（fc_run.sh:161–174、agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md），走批量链则按“有残留 state、没有 DONE”记 SKIPPED（day2_train_chain.sh:94），所以重训前先移走这个目录
ep1（mf4090）从第 5000 万步起训练慢了一倍：前 4000 万步除第一段（4 分 53 秒，含编译）外每 1000 万步 3 分 17–20 秒，到 50,001,920 步那一段 6 分 22 秒，之后 7 分 17 秒到 7 分 25 秒（results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt）。第一份含 NaN 的 checkpoint 正好是 50,001,920 步（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt）变慢与出 NaN 重合，只记重合、不写因果；旧页面“接触计算变重”的推测撤回。ml40 的截断复现也一样：L0（v0.4.0）到 30,001,152 步那一段从 3:18 变成 6:33，之后 7:23–7:27，从这份起 checkpoint 含 NaN；L1（#185）除含编译的第一段外一直 3:09–3:10、全部有限；L3（v0.4.0、fp32 存储）从第一份 10,000,384 步起就含 NaN，除含编译的第一段（5:27）外每段 7:24–7:28（results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,3}_ml40.txt）。出分时间仍按区间报；有没有发散以 checkpoint 扫描为准（stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py，看守用 --watch）
ep1 vacuum_left_lean_02 斜靠椅背，Tier 1 间隙 −22.5 mm不是平放，看回放确认，不改数据。椅背是方盒支撑面（见第一行）。Tier 1 参考里 vacuum 385 / 747 帧低于地面超过 5 mm（最深 23.8 mm）；同样穿地的 ep17（123 / 440 帧，最深 16.8 mm）、ep22（136 / 719 帧，13.8 mm）都训完、没有发散（results/diverge/support_penetration{,_floor_controls}.txt）
ep16 spray_bottle_left_desk_ground_02（半径 0.16 m、路程 5.27 m，只有左手接触，左 / 右 184 / 0 帧）训练没出 NaN，但最终平均回合长度只有 118.7、回报 15.25，同一段另外四条是 374–552、68–133（results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt）评测 MP-SR 0、chord 0、SP-SR 1 / 4096，ADD-AUC 0.255 低于不动基线 0.266，平均位置误差 63.7 cm、朝向 85.4°（results/day2/batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt、results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt）。按判据不通过，不是发散问题；和其他失败序列一起另写改进计划（见下一步）。单手、路程长是不是学不起来的原因，未查
ep29 rolling_luggage_left_foot_push_06 左右手接触帧都是 0（两档）这条本来就是用左脚推：Tier 1 双手全程离物体超过 10 cm，物体动的帧里 61% 左脚在 5 cm 内。不调接触阈值，调大会把 Tier 2 噪声造成的假接触当真（results/luggage_contact.txt，9/27 更正）。第 1 批照原配置训完，按判据通过：MP-SR 0.174、SP-SR 0.989、ADD-AUC 0.658（不动基线 0.231；results/day2/batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt）
Tier 2 的 ep7 iron_round_table_02、ep20 rice_cooker_clockwise_04 陷进桌面约 2 cm（v5.4 下 −20.7 mm、−22.1 mm；agent-doc/progress-2026-09-25.md）不穿地约束只管地面；先看训练是否受影响。ep7 的桌面在 Tier 1 的支撑面文件里是方盒，v0.4.0 与 aee95f2 会建成整块 1 m 立方体；Tier 2 用的支撑面文件还没查。导入方式定下来之后再看
ep17 traffic_cone_ground_table_02 在 Tier 2 观测里第 0 帧就低于地面 23.5 mm地面平面与重建的固有误差，被不穿地约束抬起约 14 mm，记录不处理
ep11 源帧 901，官方样例 CSV 里 e000011 只有 900 帧（第 0–899 帧）训练照用 901 帧；写打包脚本时按打包器的检查确认多出的这一帧怎么处理（未实测；帧数出自 kit 事实摘录与冒烟日志）
16 / 30 条 Tier 1 序列的官方支撑面文件是空的 Xform（没有 Cube / Cylinder 碰撞体），FlashCHORD 建场景直接报错这些序列训练时加 scene.support=false，地面保留；全部 30 条都能建场景（results/day2/palm_frame_probe_all.txt，jev 0.83）。其余 14 条：圆柱 11 条，方盒 3 条（ep1、ep7、ep28）。10/1 自行定下继续这样处理（决定记录），邮件第 2 版里降成一句告知

评测与运行环境

情况处理
评测器只要有一个世界被判终止就整条报错：evaluation must disable task failure termination before scoring the full reference。位姿非有限的世界只把位姿清零、打警告，终止判断仍按全部世界算。v0.4.0（harness.py:525–526）与 aee95f2（harness.py:580–581）相同（results/batch1/termination_facts.txt）第 1 批里 ep1 的 3 项评测分别有 2445、2387、2342 个世界（共 4096）位姿非有限，随后都报这个错（results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt）；9/29 ep7 冒烟的 256 世界 rollout 里有 172 个（results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt）。两次都是方盒序列。官方每条只评 1 个世界：那个世界若被判终止，这条交不出轨迹，而每次提交要有全部 30 条。10/1 自行定下：只有数值发散会触发，含 NaN 的 policy 不导出，报这个错就退到同一条 run 里更早的有限 checkpoint；这一问不再写进邮件（决定记录）
真实时长回放时，G1 绑定参考要求每帧 palm 与关节 FK 相差 ≤ 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad，插值帧过不了全部 30 条重采样后实测最大 4.78 mm / 2.84e-2 rad（ep10 white_desk_lower_to_ground_10，jev 0.90）；只放宽这两个校验容差：ep20 用 2 mm / 5e-3 rad，Day 2 统一用 15 mm / 0.086 rad（最大值的 3 倍），远小于 15 cm / 1.5 rad 的跟踪门槛
训练中把 checkpoint 同步上传成 W&B artifact，客户端核验 token 连不上服务器，训练进程直接退出长跑一律 V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0：指标照常进 W&B，checkpoint 只存本地（snack 第 1 次尝试，jev 0.92）
训练链跑着的时候同步代码，会改掉来历、换掉链后面才调用的脚本链开跑写非空的 runs/.chain-active-*，scripts/dev/remote 见到就拒绝同步（只读查询直接 ssh）；链在每条 run 前比对来历，变了就停。链在跑时要在服务器上跑仓内脚本：只用标准库的 Python 用 scripts/dev/pipe-py 经 ssh stdin 送过去执行；要在一条链之后接一个队列，用 stages/policy/queue_after.sh，先复制到 runs/tools/ 再跑（记 md5），它自己也写标记
stages/policy/fc_config_check.sh 走 fc_run.sh 的打印模式，会先建一个空的 runs/logs/<run>.log（fc_run.sh:47）看 run 日志判断开没开跑、挂的哪个导入器时，空日志不算数；stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py 把还没有训练段开始行的 run 记“未跑”
mf4090 上 400 万 buffer 训练时整卡读数 23.8 GB（卡 24 GB）XLA 没开预分配、按需扩池，整卡读数反映池子大小；放不放得下只看日志里有没有 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED / out of memory。Day 2 第一次开链的峰值上限与此不一致，见 Day 2
Mac 往两台服务器上传几 MB 的文件会卡住（换不复用的新连接也卡，卡住时复用连接跟着不通）；下载正常必须上传时切成 512 KB 分块逐块传，在服务器上拼回、核 sha256；卡住的复用连接用 ssh -O exit 重置。诊断尽量在服务器上做，只把 results/ 回流到 Mac
在训练用的卡上开查看器会拖慢训练：9/29 10:32–11:46 在 ml40 上开了 3 个 viser、录了 3 条 rerun，ep0 每 1000 万步从 3 分 42 秒变成 5 分，多用约 20 分钟看效果优先录短的 rerun .rrd（每条约 3 分钟 GPU）拿回 Mac 看，或等训完再开 viser；训练中要开就限时（results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt）
旧版留下的 *_g1_support.usda会被优先加载且已不对齐，重定向前删除
mf4090 连 GitHub 时连接被重置在 Mac 上打 git bundle，传过去再 fetch

Special cases

As of 14:40 on 9/30. Two tables: sequences and references, then evaluation and runtime. The box-support row concerns three episodes, ep1, ep7 and ep28, and the submission as well.

Sequences and references

CaseHandling
v0.4.0 builds box supports as solid cubes with 1 m edges (the chair back in ep1 vacuum, the tabletop in ep7 iron, the two chair-back plates in ep28 squeegee). v0.4.0 scene/support.py:85–86, 114–116 reads only size from a USD Cube and ignores xformOp:scale; built for real, all of them come out with half extents (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) m (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt). aee95f2, the runtime the kit names, does the same (results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt). With that geometry the reference object sits inside the block from frame 0: ep7 is embedded more than 5 mm in 896 of 896 frames, about 49 cm deep at frame 0 (jev did not verify this one; the numbers are copied from the script output); ep1 in 519 of 747 frames; ep28 in 608 and 651 of 988 frames for its two plates (results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt). None of the three has a batch 1 result: every ep1 checkpoint from 50,001,920 steps on contains NaN and its 3 evaluations failed, the watcher stopped ep28 at 60,002,304 steps, and ep7 was skipped after its smoke runUpstream 8c778f905c (#185, 12:11 on 9/29) fixes it by building a Cube as size times xformOp:scale. The patched copy on ml40 (V2D_FC_SRC, see Status) builds the ep7 tabletop with half extents (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) m (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt). Final verdict of the GPU controls under the registered criteria (14:35, results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt): H1′ is supported. In the ep7 smoke runs (4M steps, L40S, fp16 storage), 5 of 8 seeds hit NaN with v0.4.0 and 0 of 8 with #185 (paired seeds 43–50), one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128; ep1 truncated at 60M steps hits NaN with v0.4.0 and stays finite with #185 (L40S, single seed, supporting evidence only). What this covers: v0.4.0 building the boxes as 1 m cubes is the trigger of the ep7 smoke divergence, and the single-seed ep1 evidence points the same way; ep28 is registered as description only; how much fp16 storage and the BN statistics each contribute is not established. The 10/1 kit pins the evaluation runtime to 8c778f9 (which includes #185), and the three episodes are retrained once per tier on the #185 geometry (box plan v2 deployed: the 4 runs on mf4090 started at 06:43 on 10/2, the 2 on ml40 follow Tier 2 segment C). Details on Divergence investigation
Under the #185 geometry (the plates' labeled sizes), the ep28 squeegee reference still passes through both 2 cm plates: vertices come out the other side in 239 and 209 frames (results/diverge/support_penetration.txt)Registered as description only, not used to judge any hypothesis: the #185 smoke run ends at episode length 87.0 and return 4.46; truncated at 60M steps, all 6 checkpoints are finite, ending at episode length 295.8 and return 54.72 (results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag.txt, ckpt_nonfinite_L2_ml40.txt). Look at the reference before retraining. On mf4090, runs/flash_chord/t1-ep28-w2048-cap4m-mf4090 still holds the states at 50,001,920 and 60,002,304 steps (the matching policies both contain non-finite parameters) and no DONE; fc_run removed the STOP marker when it exited (fc_run.sh:211–212). Rerunning the same run name directly with fc_run.sh resumes from the NaN state at 60,002,304 steps (fc_run.sh:161–174, agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md), and the batch chain marks it SKIPPED for leftover state without DONE (day2_train_chain.sh:94), so move the folder away before retraining
ep1 (mf4090) trained at half speed from 50M steps on: 3 min 17–20 s per 10M steps up to 40M apart from the first segment (4 min 53 s, including compilation), 6 min 22 s for the segment ending at 50,001,920, then 7 min 17 s to 7 min 25 s to the end (results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt). The first checkpoint with NaN is exactly the one at 50,001,920 steps (results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt)The slowdown and the NaN coincide; we record the coincidence and claim no cause. The earlier guess on this site, that contact computation got heavier, is withdrawn. The truncated runs on ml40 show the same: L0 (v0.4.0) goes from 3:18 to 6:33 in the segment ending at 30,001,152 steps and then 7:23–7:27, with NaN in every checkpoint from that one on; L1 (#185) stays at 3:09–3:10 after the first segment, which includes compilation, and finite throughout; L3 (v0.4.0, fp32 storage) has NaN from its first checkpoint at 10,000,384 steps, and after the first segment (5:27, including compilation) every segment takes 7:24–7:28 (results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt, ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,3}_ml40.txt). Expected results are still reported as a range; whether a run diverged is decided by the checkpoint scan (stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py, --watch for a watcher)
ep1 vacuum leaning on a chair back, Tier 1 gap −22.5 mmIt does not rest flat; confirm on replay and leave the data unchanged. The chair back is a box support (see the first row). In the Tier 1 reference the vacuum is more than 5 mm below the floor in 385 of 747 frames (deepest 23.8 mm); ep17 (123 of 440 frames, deepest 16.8 mm) and ep22 (136 of 719 frames, 13.8 mm) go through the floor too, and both trained to the end without diverging (results/diverge/support_penetration{,_floor_controls}.txt)
ep16 spray bottle (spray_bottle_left_desk_ground_02; radius 0.16 m, path 5.27 m, left hand only, 184 / 0 contact frames left / right) trained without NaN, but ends with a mean episode length of only 118.7 and return 15.25, against 374–552 and 68–133 for the other four runs of the segment (results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt)Evaluation: MP-SR 0, chord 0, SP-SR 1 of 4096, ADD-AUC 0.255 below the do-nothing baseline of 0.266, mean position error 63.7 cm and orientation error 85.4° (results/day2/batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt, results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt). It fails the criteria; it is not a divergence. It goes into the improvement plans with the other failing sequences (see Next steps). Whether one-handed contact and the long path are why it does not learn has not been checked
ep29 luggage has zero contact frames for both hands (both tiers)The sequence is a left-foot push: in Tier 1 both hands stay more than 10 cm from the object, and the left foot is within 5 cm in 61% of the frames where the object moves. Do not raise the contact threshold; that would turn Tier 2 noise into fake contacts (results/luggage_contact.txt, corrected 9/27). Batch 1 trained it with the unchanged recipe and it passes the criteria: MP-SR 0.174, SP-SR 0.989, ADD-AUC 0.658 (do-nothing baseline 0.231; results/day2/batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt)
In Tier 2 the ep7 iron and the ep20 rice cooker sink about 2 cm into the tabletop (−20.7 mm and −22.1 mm under v5.4; agent-doc/progress-2026-09-25.md)The no-penetration constraint only covers the floor; check whether training is affected first. In the Tier 1 support file the ep7 tabletop is a box, which v0.4.0 and aee95f2 build as a whole 1 m cube; the support files Tier 2 uses have not been checked. Revisit once the importer question is settled
ep17 traffic cone already 23.5 mm below the floor at frame 0 in the Tier 2 observationAn inherent error between the ground plane and the reconstruction; the no-penetration constraint lifts it about 14 mm. Recorded, not fixed
ep11 has 901 source frames, but the official sample CSV has 900 frames for e000011 (frames 0–899)Training keeps all 901 frames; when writing the packing script, check how the packer treats the extra frame (not tested yet; frame counts from the kit excerpts and the smoke log)
For 16 of the 30 Tier 1 sequences the official support file is an empty Xform (no Cube or Cylinder collider), and FlashCHORD fails to build the sceneTrain these sequences with scene.support=false; the floor stays. All 30 sequences build a scene that way (results/day2/palm_frame_probe_all.txt, jev 0.83). Of the other 14, 11 use cylinders and 3 use boxes (ep1, ep7, ep28). On 10/1 we decided to keep this handling (decision record), and email draft v2 mentions it in one sentence

Evaluation and runtime

CaseHandling
The evaluator raises an error for the whole run as soon as one world is marked terminated: evaluation must disable task failure termination before scoring the full reference. Worlds with non-finite poses only get their poses zeroed and a warning; termination is still checked over all worlds. v0.4.0 (harness.py:525–526) and aee95f2 (harness.py:580–581) are the same (results/batch1/termination_facts.txt)In batch 1 the 3 evaluations of ep1 had 2445, 2387 and 2342 worlds (of 4096) with non-finite poses, and each then raised this error (results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt); the 256-world rollout of the ep7 smoke run on 9/29 had 172 (results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt). Both are box sequences. The official evaluation uses one world per episode: if that world is marked terminated, the episode yields no trajectory, and every submission needs all 30. Decided on 10/1: only a numerical blow-up triggers it, a policy with NaN is never exported, and this error means falling back to an earlier finite checkpoint of the same run; the question is dropped from the email (decision record)
At real-time playback, the G1 binding requires every palm frame to match the joint FK within 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad, which interpolated frames failMeasured over all 30 resampled sequences, the largest deviation is 4.78 mm / 2.84e-2 rad (ep10 white desk, jev 0.90). Only these two check tolerances are relaxed: 2 mm / 5e-3 rad for ep20, and 15 mm / 0.086 rad (3× the maximum) for Day 2, far below the 15 cm / 1.5 rad tracking limits
Uploading a checkpoint as a W&B artifact during training: when the client cannot reach the server to verify the token, the training process exitsLong runs always set V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0: metrics still go to W&B, checkpoints stay local (first snack attempt, jev 0.92)
Syncing code while a training chain runs changes the recorded source and swaps scripts the chain calls laterA chain writes a non-empty runs/.chain-active-* when it starts, and scripts/dev/remote refuses to sync while one exists (read-only queries go over plain ssh); the chain compares the source before every run and stops if it changed. To run a repository script on a server while a chain runs: a stdlib-only Python script goes over ssh stdin with scripts/dev/pipe-py; to queue work behind a chain, copy stages/policy/queue_after.sh to runs/tools/ first (recording its md5) and run it from there, and it writes its own marker
stages/policy/fc_config_check.sh goes through the print mode of fc_run.sh, which creates an empty runs/logs/<run>.log first (fc_run.sh:47)When a run log is used to tell whether a run started or which importer it mounted, an empty log counts for nothing; stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py marks a run without a training-segment start line as not run
With a 4M buffer on mf4090 the whole card reads 23.8 GB (24 GB card)XLA runs without preallocation and grows its pool on demand, so the whole-card reading reflects the pool size; whether the run fits is decided only by RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED / out of memory in the log. The first Day 2 chain used a peak limit that disagreed with this, see Day 2
Uploads of a few MB from the Mac to either server stall (a fresh, unshared connection stalls too, and the shared connection hangs with it); downloads are fineWhen an upload is unavoidable, send it in 512 KB chunks, join them on the server and check the sha256; reset a hung shared connection with ssh -O exit. Diagnose on the servers where possible and bring only results/ back to the Mac
A viewer on a training card slows training: from 10:32 to 11:46 on 9/29, three viser viewers and three rerun recordings on ml40 took ep0 from 3 min 42 s to 5 min per 10M steps, about 20 minutes lostTo look at a policy, prefer a short rerun .rrd recording (about 3 GPU minutes each) viewed on the Mac, or open viser after training; time-box it if it must run during training (results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt)
Leftover *_g1_support.usda from the old retargeterIt is loaded first and is misaligned; delete it before retargeting

训练实验

Day 1 之后做了三次实验、一次查证；官方 kit 发布后 Day 2 改为固定逐帧；第 1 批 21 条 Tier 1 的最后一项评测在 9/30 12:35 结束：通过 9 条、不通过 9 条、无效 3 条（ep7 没有正式训练，ep1 评测失败，ep28 止损后没评）。这三条另开了发散排查：v0.4.0 把它们的方盒支撑面建成 1 m 立方体；GPU 对照 9/30 14:35 跑完，按登记判据的最终判定，这是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发（H1′ 支持，8 个配对种子，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128），ep1 只有单种子旁证，ep28 只描述。10/1 00:49 起跑 Tier 2 第 1 批（27 条），训练链加了发散检测，见 Tier 2 第 1 批。每次都先登记判据再跑，结论由脚本按判据给出。页面上的承重结论都经 jev 核对，P(支持) ≥ 0.7 才算核实，分数写在旁边；没过的标“未核实”。

  • 任务一览30 条任务的英文名、官方任务原文、人类示范视频与 G1 参考动画30 条
  • Day 1b 逐帧诊断ep20 的 ADD-AUC 丢在哪一段，官方样例作对照T1 T2 T4 触发
  • snack 满配对照官方配方在官方样例上能不能过线不通过（边缘）
  • 回放速度对照1.67 倍速对真实时长，转动落后量有没有差别无差别
  • 官方评测与提交kit 定下的评测口径、提交流程、还缺什么固定逐帧
  • Day 2新序列上的逐帧满配能不能过线，两种回放怎么选2/8 通过

9/30 08:57–10:35发散排查（CPU）读代码：BatchNorm 统计量不过滤非有限值，梯度非有限时参数跳过更新、统计量照写；10:35 查到 v0.4.0 把方盒支撑面建成 1 m 立方体
9/30 11:08–14:35发散排查（GPU，ml40）ep7 冒烟配对种子 43–50（8 个）：v0.4.0 5/8 发散，换上 #185 0/8，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128，14:35 按登记判据判定 H1′ 支持；H3 拒绝，H4 在 4 个种子时已拒绝（fp32 存储也 2/4）；L 组单种子旁证：ep1 在 v0.4.0 下 fp16、fp32 都发散，换上 #185 不发散
10/1 00:39–00:50Tier 2 第 1 批开链前核对两台 PREFLIGHT_OK、测试全过、配置核对意外差异 0、四段 PRECHECK_OK；ml40 00:49、mf4090 00:50 开链
10/1 00:49 起（进行中）Tier 2 第 1 批08:00：冒烟 9/10 通过（ep10 W&B 网络超时，改到 C 段）；正式训完 6 条，最终 policy 都没有非有限参数，看守没有止损；还没评

第 1 批的判定见 results/day2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_batch1{a,b}.txt；12:35 是 mf4090 B 段最后一项评测（ep29 最终 checkpoint）结果文件的修改时间；文件抬头的 run_at 是开始时间 12:32（原为北京时间，已换算），见 results/day2/eval_t1-ep29-w2048-cap4m-mf4090_s349999104.txt。发散排查从 08:57 计划登记（af261bf）、ep28 看守开始算，见 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md，最终判定 results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt（4 个种子时的中途判定 results/diverge/diag_verdict_seeds43_46.txt 留档），ep28 止损 results/diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log.txt。

怎么读这些分数

“不动基线”是物体一直停在第 0 帧参考位姿时的 ADD-AUC，随序列和回放方式变（ep20 是 0.471，snack 样例 0.566，Day 2 的 ep13 只有 0.165），及格线用各自的基线。分数只在同一序列、同一评测回放内可比。

jev 核对：results/site/jev_claims_sop_page.txt，22 条全部核实，3 道对照题方向正确；ml40 冒烟链与 ep10 的 2 条另行补核（…_ep10.txt），也都核实。第一轮（…_run1.txt）有两条没过，处理见snack 满配对照与官方评测与提交。9/29 这次更新的 12 条新论断另核一遍（…_0929.txt，规格 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-sop-page.claims.json），全部核实（0.91–0.99）。第 2 轮有一道假对照题失灵：它考 900 与 901 这种相邻数字（0.33，门槛 0.3），而数值比较本来就不交给 jev；换成非数值的对照题后第 3 轮 3 道都正常，三轮的论断分数相差不超过 0.02（…_0929_run1.txt、…_run2.txt）。9/30 这次更新的新论断另核一遍（results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt，规格 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-sop-page.claims.json，第 5 轮，14:36）：21 条里 20 条核实（0.79–0.98），5 道对照题正常（真对照 0.98，假对照 0.01–0.02）；最终判定那条（8 个种子 R1 5/8、R3′ 0/8，p = 0.0128，H1′ 支持）0.98。“v0.4.0 几何下 ep7 iron_round_table_02 896 帧全埋、第 0 帧深约 49 cm”五轮是 0.62、0.39、0.65、0.62、0.54，未核实，页面上照写脚本输出并标未核实。前四轮留档（…_0930_run1.txt–…_run4.txt）：R2 观测块那条初稿写“位置块全 NaN”，不准（只取自参考的未来根位置块有限），改准后 0.78–0.84；ep1 节奏那条初稿漏了第一段含编译的 4:53，改准后 0.97。Day 2 的结果与 kit 的事实沿用各自的核对（results/day2/jev_claims_results.txt、results/submission/jev_claims_kit.txt）；第 1 批与发散排查同样沿用各自的核对（results/day2/jev_claims_batch1a_ml40.txt、…_batch1a_mf4090.txt、…_batch1b_ml40.txt、results/batch1/jev_claims_failure_analysis.txt、results/diverge/jev_claims_divergence.txt）。表里的数字不交给 jev，逐个对过判定脚本的输出。10/1 08:00 这次更新的 14 条新论断另核一遍（results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_1001.txt，第 2 轮），全部核实（0.83–0.99），见 Tier 2 第 1 批。

Training experiments

After Day 1 we ran three experiments and one fact-check; after the official kit came out, Day 2 switched to frame-by-frame playback only. The last evaluation of batch 1's 21 Tier 1 runs ended at 12:35 on 9/30: 9 pass, 9 fail, 3 are invalid (ep7 never got its full training run, ep1's evaluation failed, ep28 was stopped and not evaluated). These three got their own divergence investigation: v0.4.0 builds their box supports as 1 m cubes. The GPU controls finished at 14:35 on 9/30, and the final verdict under the registered criteria is that this triggers the divergence of the ep7 smokes (H1′ supported, 8 paired seeds, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128); ep1 has only single-seed supporting evidence, and ep28 is description only. Tier 2 batch 1 (27 runs) has been running since 00:49 on 10/1, with divergence detection added to the training chain; see Tier 2 batch 1. Every experiment registered its criteria before running, and a script produced the verdict from them. The load-bearing conclusions on these pages were checked with jev: a claim counts as verified at P(supported) ≥ 0.7, with the score shown next to it; anything below is marked unverified.

  • Full-recipe snack controlWhether the official recipe passes on the official sampleFail (marginal)
  • Playback speed comparison1.67× against real time: does the rotation lag differNo difference

How to read these scores

The do-nothing baseline is the ADD-AUC of an object that stays at its frame-0 reference pose. It changes with the sequence and the playback (0.471 for ep20, 0.566 for the snack sample, only 0.165 for Day 2's ep13), and every pass bar uses its own baseline. Scores compare only within one sequence and one evaluation playback.

jev: results/site/jev_claims_sop_page.txt, all 22 claims verified and the 3 controls behaved; 2 later claims about the ml40 smoke chain and ep10 were checked separately (…_ep10.txt) and verified too. Two claims failed in the first round (…_run1.txt); see Full-recipe snack control and Official evaluation and submission for how they were handled. The 12 new claims of the 9/29 update were checked separately (…_0929.txt, spec agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-sop-page.claims.json): all verified (0.91–0.99). In the second round one false control misfired: it tested neighbouring numbers, 900 against 901 (0.33 against a 0.3 limit), and numeric comparison is not jev's job to begin with; with a non-numeric control instead, all 3 controls behaved in the third round, and the claim scores differ by at most 0.02 across the three rounds (…_0929_run1.txt, …_run2.txt). The new claims of the 9/30 update were checked separately as well (results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt, spec agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-sop-page.claims.json, round 5, 14:36): 20 of 21 claims verified (0.79–0.98) and the 5 controls behaved (true control 0.98, false controls 0.01–0.02); the final verdict claim (8 seeds, R1 5 of 8 against R3′ 0 of 8, p = 0.0128, H1′ supported) scored 0.98. "Under the v0.4.0 geometry the ep7 iron is buried in all 896 frames, about 49 cm deep at frame 0" scored 0.62, 0.39, 0.65, 0.62 and 0.54 over the five rounds and is unverified, so the page quotes the script output and marks it unverified. The first four rounds are kept (…_0930_run1.txt to …_run4.txt): the first draft of the R2 observation-block claim said every position block was NaN, which is wrong (the future root position block, taken only from the reference, is finite), and after the fix it scored 0.78–0.84; the first draft of the ep1 pace claim left out the first interval of 4:53, which includes compilation, and after the fix it scored 0.97. The Day 2 results and the kit facts keep their own checks (results/day2/jev_claims_results.txt, results/submission/jev_claims_kit.txt), and so do batch 1 and the divergence investigation (results/day2/jev_claims_batch1a_ml40.txt, …_batch1a_mf4090.txt, …_batch1b_ml40.txt, results/batch1/jev_claims_failure_analysis.txt, results/diverge/jev_claims_divergence.txt). Numbers in tables are not left to jev; each was compared with the verdict scripts' output. The 14 new claims of the 08:00 update on 10/1 were checked separately (results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_1001.txt, round 2): all verified (0.83–0.99); see Tier 2 batch 1.

任务一览

Track 2 的 30 条 episode，每条一张卡：英文任务名（数据集的序列名去掉日期前缀）、官方英文任务原文、两段视频。左边是 exo 相机里的人类示范，右边是重定向到 G1 之后的参考动作，也就是 policy 要去跟的东西。

  • 人类示范取自数据集的 exo 相机视频（这一路视频三档逐字节相同），经重编码，2 倍速、640 宽；数据集许可 CC BY 4.0。
  • G1 参考用的是 FlashCHORD 回放脚本的运动学参考模式：每一帧直接把机器人和物体放到参考位姿，不跑物理，所以它说明的是“要做成什么样”，不代表 policy 的表现，也不说明这条参考在物理上做得到。支撑面只画了顶面（蓝色），桌子本身不在仿真场景里。ep1、ep7、ep28 的方盒支撑面按上游 #185 的几何画；v0.4.0 会把它们建成 1 m 立方体，见发散排查。
  • 卡片上的判定与 MP-SR、SP-SR 是 Tier 1、逐帧满配、最终 checkpoint、本地 4096 个世界的结果，判定标准见第 1 批批量，不是排行榜分数；来源 results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt 与 results/day2/batch_verdict_*.txt。
  • 素材清单 results/site/media_manifest.txt；生成脚本 stages/evaluate/site_media_exo.sh、stages/policy/site_media_ref.sh、stages/policy/site_ref_replay.py、tools/site_task_gallery.py，计划 agent-doc/plan-site-media-2026-09-30.md。视频进入视口时才加载。

Task gallery

All 30 Track 2 episodes, one card each: the English task name (the dataset sequence name without its date prefix), the official English task text, and two videos. On the left is the human demonstration from the exo camera; on the right is the reference motion after retargeting to the G1, which is what the policy has to track.

  • The human demos come from the dataset's exo camera video (byte-identical across the three tiers), re-encoded at 2× speed and 640 px wide; the dataset is licensed CC BY 4.0.
  • The G1 reference uses the kinematic-reference mode of the FlashCHORD replay script: every frame places the robot and the object directly at the reference pose, with no physics. It shows what the task should look like; it says nothing about how a policy performs or whether the reference is physically feasible. Support surfaces are drawn by their top only (blue); the tables themselves are not part of the simulated scene. The box supports of ep1, ep7 and ep28 use the upstream #185 geometry; v0.4.0 builds them as 1 m cubes, see Divergence investigation.
  • The verdicts, MP-SR and SP-SR on the cards are Tier 1, frame-playback full recipe, final checkpoint, 4096 local worlds; the criteria are on Batch 1. They are not leaderboard scores. Sources: results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt and results/day2/batch_verdict_*.txt.
  • Media list in results/site/media_manifest.txt; generated by stages/evaluate/site_media_exo.sh, stages/policy/site_media_ref.sh, stages/policy/site_ref_replay.py and tools/site_task_gallery.py, plan agent-doc/plan-site-media-2026-09-30.md. Videos load when they scroll into view.

Day 1b 逐帧诊断

Day 1 的评测只给汇总分数。这一步把三个 policy 的评测 rollout 存下来逐帧拆开：ep20 满配（主）、ep20 100 万 buffer、官方样例 snack 100 万（对照）。9/27 19:53–20:24 在 mf4090 上跑，同一张 4090、4096 世界。七道有效性闸（形状、包装前后一致、重算 ADD-AUC 一致、朝向误差一致、参考的已知答案、逐世界成功率、几何自检）三个 policy 全过。

ep20 满配ep20 100 万snack 100 万
全程 ADD-AUC（不动）0.4315（0.4708）0.4213（0.4708）0.5410（0.5662）
第 1 段贡献差（操作前静止）−0.027−0.033−0.032
第 2 段贡献差（操作窗口）+0.032+0.022+0.006
第 3 段贡献差（操作后）−0.045−0.0380.000
中位净转动比（需要）0.70（0.72）0.76（0.72）0.56（0.55）
操作段 palm 误差中位5.1 cm9.2 cm62.7 cm
参考手在接触帧上离物体（中位）右 5.9 cm右 5.9 cm左 2.6、右 2.8 cm
成功世界（4096 个里）3285620

“贡献差”是这一段对全程 ADD-AUC 的贡献减去“物体不动”在同一段的贡献，三段相加等于全程差；负数表示这一段不如不动。

触发了什么（事先登记的数值条件） jev 0.91

  • T1 触发：非操作段（第 1、3 段）占全程 ADD-AUC 差距的 182%（主）、126%（对照），都 ≥ 50%，按登记归到配方层：查接近与抓取、reset 与 VOC 设置。
  • T2 触发：中位净转动比 0.70，低于第 420 帧不越门槛所需的 0.72，下一步做回放速度对照（已做，见回放速度对照）。
  • T3 未触发：操作段 palm 误差中位 5.1 cm，低于 7.5 cm。
  • T4 触发：参考手在接触帧上离物体 5.9 cm，对照 2.6 cm，差 ≥ 2 cm，下一步查重定向。限定：两条的物体与抓法不同，这个差也可能来自物体形状。

描述（不设阈值）

  • Day 1 撤回的“没跟上转动”确实不成立：ep20 满配 82% 的世界净转过参考的一半，中位转了 70%（第 209 帧到末帧净转 212°，参考 307°）。
  • ep20 满配的失分分三块：操作前就把 rice cooker 预转约 20°（第 209 帧朝向误差中位 20°，第 1 段 −0.027）；操作窗口内比不动好（+0.032）；操作结束后物体停在比参考少转约 75° 的位置（第 552 帧朝向误差中位 74.8°，第 3 段 −0.045）。T1 把第 1、3 段算在一起，其中第 3 段实际是转动不足留下的残差。
  • snack 100 万的失分几乎全在静止前缀：盒子在前 446 帧被碰动（位置误差均值 1.4 cm，不动为 0）。

记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md；报告 results/day1/diag_*.txt；触发判定 results/day1/diag_triggers.txt。

Day 1b frame-by-frame diagnosis

The Day 1 evaluation only reports aggregates. This step saved the evaluation rollouts of three policies and took them apart frame by frame: full-recipe ep20 (main), ep20 with a 1M buffer, and the official snack sample with a 1M buffer (control). It ran on mf4090 from 19:53 to 20:24 on 9/27, on the same 4090 with 4096 worlds. All three policies passed the seven validity gates (shapes, identical results with and without the wrapper, recomputed ADD-AUC, orientation error, known answers of the reference, per-world success, geometric self-check).

ep20 full recipeep20 1Msnack 1M
Full-run ADD-AUC (do-nothing)0.4315 (0.4708)0.4213 (0.4708)0.5410 (0.5662)
Segment 1 contribution gap (static, before manipulation)−0.027−0.033−0.032
Segment 2 (manipulation window)+0.032+0.022+0.006
Segment 3 (after manipulation)−0.045−0.0380.000
Median net rotation ratio (required)0.70 (0.72)0.76 (0.72)0.56 (0.55)
Median palm error, manipulation segment5.1 cm9.2 cm62.7 cm
Reference hand to object at contact frames (median)right 5.9 cmright 5.9 cmleft 2.6, right 2.8 cm
Successful worlds (of 4096)3285620

A segment's contribution gap is its contribution to the full-run ADD-AUC minus the do-nothing contribution over the same segment; the three add up to the full-run gap, and a negative value means the segment did worse than not moving.

What fired (registered numeric conditions) jev 0.91

  • T1 fired: segments 1 and 3 account for 182% of the ADD-AUC gap for the main policy and 126% for the control, both ≥ 50%, so by the registration this is a recipe-level issue: check approach and grasp, reset and VOC settings.
  • T2 fired: the median net rotation ratio is 0.70, below the 0.72 needed to stay inside the limit at frame 420; the next step was a playback speed comparison (done, see Playback speed comparison).
  • T3 did not fire: the median palm error in the manipulation segment is 5.1 cm, below 7.5 cm.
  • T4 fired: the reference hand is 5.9 cm from the object at contact frames against 2.6 cm for the control, a gap of at least 2 cm; the next step is to check retargeting. Caveat: the two sequences differ in object and grip, so the gap may come from object shape.

Description (no thresholds)

  • The claim withdrawn after Day 1, that the policy does not follow the rotation, is indeed wrong: 82% of full-recipe ep20 worlds turn more than half of the reference's net rotation, with a median of 70% (212° net from frame 209 to the end, against 307° in the reference).
  • The full-recipe ep20 policy loses in three places: it pre-turns the cooker about 20° before manipulation starts (median orientation error 20° at frame 209, segment 1 −0.027); inside the manipulation window it beats doing nothing (+0.032); after manipulation the object rests about 75° short of the reference (median orientation error 74.8° at frame 552, segment 3 −0.045). T1 counts segments 1 and 3 together; segment 3 is really the leftover of the under-rotation.
  • The 1M snack policy loses almost everything in the static prefix: the box gets bumped during the first 446 frames (mean position error 1.4 cm, zero for doing nothing).

Record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md; reports results/day1/diag_*.txt; trigger verdict results/day1/diag_triggers.txt.

snack 满配对照

目的是分清 Day 1 的失败出在配方与环境，还是出在 ep20 这条数据。用官方 G1 配方，只给官方样例 parquet，1000 万 buffer、349,999,104 步、种子 42，在 ml40 上训练，4096 世界评测。判据事先登记：chord_sr > 0、maniptrans_sr > 0、ADD-AUC 高于这条序列的不动基线 0.5662；“> 0”只靠 ≤ 3 个世界，或 ADD-AUC 落在门槛 ±0.01 内，另标“边缘”。

第 1 次尝试：中途退出，未判定 jev 0.92

9/27 19:56 开始，20:01 写出第一个 checkpoint（10,000,384 步）。20:02 要把它上传成 W&B artifact 时，客户端核验 API token 连不上服务器（AuthenticationError），异常没被接住，训练进程退出。按登记不续训。第 2 次从 0 重来，关掉 checkpoint 上传（V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0，指标照常进 W&B），判据不变。

第 2 次尝试：不通过（边缘） jev 0.94

9/28 07:32 开始，一段跑完，09:32 评完。

项满配（第 2 次，ml40）100 万（Day 1，mf4090）门槛
chord_sr0.922（3775 / 4096）0> 0 → 过
maniptrans_sr0（0 / 4096）0.00024（1 个世界）> 0 → 不过
ADD-AUC0.56390.5403> 0.5662 → 不过，差 −0.0023
spider_sr_uncentered0.967—只描述
  • 按登记三项要全过，所以不通过；ADD-AUC 落在门槛 ±0.01 内，另标边缘。边缘不走 SOP 的因果分支，下一步按诊断证据定，或补一个种子。
  • 官方配方在官方样例上任务原生成功率 92%，物体位置误差比不动低约 56 cm，ADD-AUC 仍略低于不动。
不下的结论
  • 100 万与满配之差不能单归因于 buffer：两条还换了机器（4090 对 L40S），各只有一个种子（jev：“只差 buffer” 0.35、“归因于 buffer” 0.39，未核实）。
  • 满配这条的失分是否也出在静止前缀（前 440 帧里约 317 帧是上游加的前缀），目前只有 100 万那条的逐帧诊断支持，属推测（jev 0.72，证据间接）。
  • 官方样例 parquet 的 fps 列是 200，采集帧率未知，不对它的回放速度下结论。

记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md 的 A 节；判定 results/day1/verdict_snackbox_cap10m.txt；第 1 次的原始日志摘录 results/ml40/day1/snack_attempt1_*.txt。第一轮 jev 这条只有 0.66：证据只有我们自己的记录，补上原始日志、run_record 与第 2 次的配置行后重核，论断原文未改。

Full-recipe snack control

The goal is to tell whether the Day 1 failure comes from the recipe and environment or from the ep20 data. Official G1 recipe, given only the official sample parquet, 10M buffer, 349,999,104 steps, seed 42, trained on ml40 and evaluated with 4096 worlds. Criteria registered in advance: chord_sr > 0, maniptrans_sr > 0, and ADD-AUC above this sequence's do-nothing baseline of 0.5662. A "> 0" that rests on ≤ 3 worlds, or an ADD-AUC within ±0.01 of the bar, is also marked marginal.

Attempt 1: exited midway, no verdict jev 0.92

Started at 19:56 on 9/27 and wrote its first checkpoint (10,000,384 steps) at 20:01. At 20:02, when uploading it as a W&B artifact, the client could not reach the server to verify the API token (AuthenticationError); nothing caught the exception and the training process exited. As registered, it was not resumed. Attempt 2 restarted from zero with checkpoint upload off (V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0; metrics still go to W&B), with the same criteria.

Attempt 2: fail (marginal) jev 0.94

Started at 07:32 on 9/28, ran in one segment, evaluated by 09:32.

ItemFull recipe (attempt 2, ml40)1M buffer (Day 1, mf4090)Bar
chord_sr0.922 (3775 / 4096)0> 0 → pass
maniptrans_sr0 (0 / 4096)0.00024 (1 world)> 0 → fail
ADD-AUC0.56390.5403> 0.5662 → fail, −0.0023
spider_sr_uncentered0.967—Descriptive
  • As registered, all three items must pass, so this is a fail; ADD-AUC is within ±0.01 of the bar, so it is also marked marginal. A marginal result takes no causal branch of the SOP; the next step follows the diagnostic evidence or adds a seed.
  • On the official sample, the official recipe succeeds in 92% of worlds by the task-native criteria and its object position error is about 56 cm below doing nothing, yet its ADD-AUC is still slightly below doing nothing.
Conclusions we do not draw
  • The gap between the 1M and full runs cannot be put on the buffer alone: the two runs also differ in machine (4090 against L40S) and have one seed each (jev: "differs only in buffer" 0.35, "caused by the buffer" 0.39, unverified).
  • Whether the full run also loses in the static prefix (about 317 of the first 440 frames are a prefix added upstream) is supported only by the 1M run's diagnosis, so it is a guess (jev 0.72, indirect evidence).
  • The sample parquet's fps column says 200 and its capture rate is unknown, so we draw no conclusion about its playback speed.

Record: section A of agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md; verdict results/day1/verdict_snackbox_cap10m.txt; log excerpts of attempt 1 in results/ml40/day1/snack_attempt1_*.txt. In the first jev round this claim scored only 0.66 because its only evidence was our own record; after adding the raw log, the run record and the attempt 2 config line it was rechecked with the claim text unchanged.

回放速度对照

Day 1b 的 T2 触发（转动跟不上）。FlashCHORD 的 G1 配方默认逐帧回放：每个 50 Hz 控制步走一帧 30 fps 的参考，动作按 1.67 倍速播；关掉逐帧就按 fps 重采样成真实时长（ep20 从 553 帧变成 921 帧）。问题是真实时长训练能不能减小转动落后量。计划 agent-doc/plan-playback-2026-09-28.md 第 2 版，9/28 08:41 登记。

设计

  • 满配一对（ml40，1000 万 buffer）与 100 万一对（mf4090，只作描述），两种回放各训一条 ep20。
  • 主指标：关注段末的转动落后量 L，取最后三个 checkpoint 的平均；两臂之差超过事先登记的效应阈值 E 才算有差别。

结果：预算内没有差别 jev 0.96

满配一对1.67 倍速真实时长
关注段末落后量 L（三个 checkpoint）64.3 / 70.5 / 69.5，平均 68.1°75.9 / 69.5 / 72.8，平均 72.7°
关注段起点（负 = 提前转）−25.4°−21.4°
新增落后量：起转 / 停顿 / 快转+77.1 / +5.6 / +12.4+77.6 / −27.6 / +44.6
操作段 palm 误差中位4.7 cm4.5 cm
  • Δ = −4.6°。登记的 E = 139.3°，被 100 万真实时长一臂的 checkpoint 波动（69.7°）撑大了，这是登记规则的缺陷；只用满配两臂算，E = 15°，判定不变 jev 0.82。
  • 起转段新增的落后量在两种回放下几乎相同（77.1° 对 77.6°），与时间无关，按登记转 T4 与 T1。
  • 100 万真实时长一臂三个 checkpoint 都没有成功的世界，这个组合训不稳。

次要结果：真实时长那条过了 Day 1 判据 jev 0.95

ml40 评测，4096 世界1.67 倍速训练，逐帧评真实时长训练，真实时长评
不动基线0.4710.470
ADD-AUC0.4310.547
chord_sr0.790.809
MP-SR（maniptrans_sr）0.00050.887
SP-SR（spider_sr_uncentered）0.00020.844
MPPE5.2 cm3.9 cm
Day 1 判据不通过通过
  • 两条的转动落后量相同，所以 ADD-AUC 的差别不是来自跟上了转动。分段看，真实时长在操作段（+0.066 对 +0.032）和操作后（+0.053 对 −0.044）更好，操作前反而更差（−0.045 对 −0.027）；来源还没查。
  • 它按自己的速度评测。当时官方评测按什么速度回放未知，这个数不能当榜上分数的预测，但让回放速度这个问题的分量大了很多，Day 2 由此而来。9/28 晚 kit 发布后，计分按源帧逐帧对齐，训练改为固定逐帧（见官方评测与提交）。
开跑前挡下的两次失败
  • 真实时长的第一次冒烟 13 秒就失败：G1 绑定参考时逐帧要求 palm 与关节 FK 相差 ≤ 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad，不区分插值帧。实测重采样后相差 0.61 mm / 2.0e-3 rad，只把这两个校验容差放到 2 mm / 5e-3 rad。
  • 诊断试跑的同卡闸没过（包装前后 ADD-AUC 差 0.0064 > 0.005）：同一 policy 两次不包装的 256 世界评测也差 0.0057，是评测本身不逐位可复现；容差改为 0.005 × √(4096 / 世界数)，4096 世界时不变。

记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-playback.md；判定 results/playback/compare.txt、compare_10m_only.txt；过线判定 results/playback/verdict_rt_runs.txt。

Playback speed comparison

Day 1b fired T2 (the rotation lags). FlashCHORD's G1 recipe plays the reference frame by frame by default: every 50 Hz control step advances one frame of the 30 fps reference, so the motion plays at 1.67×. Turning that off resamples by fps to real time (ep20 goes from 553 to 921 frames). The question is whether training at real time reduces the rotation lag. Plan: version 2 of agent-doc/plan-playback-2026-09-28.md, registered at 08:41 on 9/28.

Design

  • A full-recipe pair (ml40, 10M buffer) and a 1M pair (mf4090, descriptive only): one ep20 run per playback.
  • Main metric: the rotation lag L at the end of the window of interest, averaged over the last three checkpoints; the arms differ only if the gap exceeds a registered effect threshold E.

Result: no difference within the budget jev 0.96

Full-recipe pair1.67×Real time
Lag L at window end (three checkpoints)64.3 / 70.5 / 69.5, mean 68.1°75.9 / 69.5 / 72.8, mean 72.7°
Lag at window start (negative = turns early)−25.4°−21.4°
Added lag: start-up / pause / fast turn+77.1 / +5.6 / +12.4+77.6 / −27.6 / +44.6
Median palm error, manipulation segment4.7 cm4.5 cm
  • Δ = −4.6°. The registered E = 139.3° was inflated by the checkpoint spread of the 1M real-time arm (69.7°), a flaw in the registered rule; computed from the two full-recipe arms only, E = 15° and the verdict is the same jev 0.82.
  • The lag added during start-up is almost the same at both speeds (77.1° against 77.6°), so it does not depend on time; as registered, the next steps are T4 and T1.
  • The 1M real-time arm has no successful world at any of its three checkpoints; that combination does not train stably.

Secondary result: the real-time run passes the Day 1 criteria jev 0.95

Day 1 criteriaFailPass
  • Both runs lag the rotation by the same amount, so the ADD-AUC gap does not come from following the turn better. By segment, real time does better in the manipulation window (+0.066 against +0.032) and after it (+0.053 against −0.044), and worse before it (−0.045 against −0.027); where the gap comes from is not checked yet.
  • It is evaluated at its own speed. At the time the official evaluation playback was unknown, so this number is no forecast of a leaderboard score. It did make the playback question much weightier, which is why Day 2 exists. Since the kit came out on the evening of 9/28, scoring aligns frame by frame to the source frames and training uses frame-by-frame playback only (see Official evaluation and submission).
Two failures caught before training
  • The first real-time smoke failed after 13 seconds: when binding the reference, G1 requires every palm frame to match the joint FK within 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad, interpolated frames included. Measured after resampling: 0.61 mm / 2.0e-3 rad. Only these two check tolerances were raised, to 2 mm / 5e-3 rad.
  • The same-card gate of the diagnosis dry run failed (ADD-AUC differs by 0.0064 > 0.005 with and without the wrapper). Two unwrapped 256-world evaluations of the same policy also differ by 0.0057, so the evaluator itself is not bit-reproducible; the tolerance became 0.005 × √(4096 / worlds), unchanged at 4096 worlds.

Record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-playback.md; verdicts results/playback/compare.txt and compare_10m_only.txt; pass check results/playback/verdict_rt_runs.txt.

官方评测与提交

9/28 19:46（kit 压缩包在官网上的修改时间）官方发布 submission kit，定下了评测口径：每条 episode 用一个 checkpoint、以确定性动作跑 1 个世界，导出的轨迹按 30 fps 源帧逐帧计分，每次提交要有全部 30 条 episode。据此训练固定用逐帧回放（用户 9/28 23:05 选定）。10/1 13:43 组委会重新发布 kit，评测运行时从 aee95f2 改钉在 8c778f9，这个版本含方盒支撑面的修复 #185；原定要问组委会的前三问因此由我们自行定下（下一节）。截至 10/2 07:00，Tier 1 有 27 条评过的逐帧 policy，Tier 2 第 1 批训完 26 条；8c778f9 兼容性验证的 mf4090 部分已通过，方盒 6 条在训；提交流程计划第 2 版已审、已部署，两台的导出链在等方盒评测结束（见下文“还缺什么”）。

10/1 的新 kit：评测运行时钉在 8c778f9

组委会 10/1 13:40 合并上游 #188，13:43 重新发布 kit（sha256 0f2f9f4b…）。README 把 FlashCHORD 运行时钉在 8c778f905c，#188 的说明写明这个版本含 Cube 支撑面的缩放修复，也就是 #185（results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt）。

  • v0.4.0 到 8c778f9 共 6 个提交（#180–#185）。FlashCHORD 下改了 11 个文件：评测代码（轨迹导出、来历摘要、两个新指标）、scene/support.py 与测试；依赖与镜像文件没有改动。训练路径上只有 training/flash_sac/evaluation.py 改了一行 import 写法，另加一个默认为 None 的字段（results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt）。
  • kit 自带的 flash_chord/scripts/evaluate_policy.py 与三份 Hydra 配置，和 8c778f9 的同名文件逐字节相同。kit 不带运行时本身，要自己装一个能导出排行榜轨迹的 FlashCHORD。
  • 新打包器换算 Track 2 位姿的做法：用第 0 帧求一个刚体变换，把评测器记下的参考对到这一档发布的输入上，逐帧误差都在 1 mm、1e-3 rad 以内才换算，否则整次打包报错（tools/pack_submission.py:78–79, 99, 118）。输入不是发布的那一份时，要用 --frame-references 给出，npz 里写 coordinate_frame=released_track2_world、frame_index [T]、pose_xyzw [T,1,7]。打包器对三档都按干净参考算本地分数，打印成 locally computed 一行。
  • 打包器缺一条计分 episode 就整份拒绝，拒收 environment_valid=false 的世界（读代码，pack_submission.py:274, 314, 399；jev 0.67，未核实），并要求所有轨迹的评测脚本与评测代码摘要相同。样例提交 22,989 行、30 条 episode、只有世界 0。

据此定下的事（决定记录 agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md，jev 9 / 9 核实，0.84–0.99）：

  • 三档的评测与导出都用 8c778f9，“运行时”一问不再问。
  • 评测器把 ReconBody 的终止阈值都改成 1e6，普通的任务失败不会让世界被判终止，只有数值发散会；已落盘的 96 份评测（393,216 个世界）没有一份报这个错。两条提交规矩（用户 10/1 采纳）：含 NaN 的 policy 不拿去导出，训练链 10/1 起已会标 NONFINITE 并跳过评测；提交时某条 episode 报 “must disable task failure termination”，就退到同一条 run 里更早的一份有限 checkpoint，这是唯一允许不用最终 checkpoint 的情形。
  • 只有地面的 16 条继续用 scene.support=false：8c778f9 遇到空的支撑面文件同样报错，关掉支撑面是它自己提供的路径；评测按 checkpoint 里的配置重建场景。
  • ep1、ep7、ep28 两档各重训一条，用 #185 的几何。

v0.4.0 训出的 checkpoint 能否在 8c778f9 里加载：mf4090 的兼容性验证 10/2 06:33–06:43 实测通过，7 行全部符合（agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md）。用 8c778f9 把 3 份已有 policy（t1-ep8 只有地面、t1-ep6 圆柱支撑面、t2-ep2 是 Tier 2）各导出 1 个世界，t1-ep8 再导出 2 个世界，加载、结构、来历都过，eval_code_sha256、eval_script_sha256 等于 8c778f9 的值。t1-ep6 在两个评测器下 4096 世界的 ADD-AUC 是 0.6335 与 0.6321，差 0.0014，chord_sr 的 |z| 是 1.18，都在登记的门限内。守卫拦下了 ep1 含 NaN 的 policy（退出码 12）；关掉守卫时评测器出错，没有产出导出。单个世界与 4096 世界的差别：t1-ep6 只导 1 个世界时 MP-SR 是 0，同一 checkpoint 4096 世界的 MP-SR 是 0.57。ml40 再导出 2 份，排在 Tier 2 C 段之后。打包在 Mac 上用 uv 跑：服务器镜像里没有 pandas，kit 也不在服务器上。

Tier 2 打包要加 --frame-references（已生成）

Tier 2 的参考是 v5.4 降噪后的轨迹，与发布的带噪输入差几厘米，不加 --frame-references，打包必然报 “do not differ by one rigid transform”。帧参考按 kit 的 npz 格式由 runs/recovery/tier2_v54 生成：v5.4 存的是 OpenCV 世界系、wxyz 顺序，与发布数据同一约定，换成 xyzw 即可。打包器对 Tier 2 也读干净参考、打印本地分数；我们的解读是 Tier 2、Tier 3 的本地分数不拿来做任何选择，打包时滤掉那一行（属解读，不是官方原文，待用户确认）。10/2 已按这个格式生成 30 条（stages/evaluate/make_frame_references.py，只读 Tier 2 的文件），都过了粗核：与发布的带噪输入相比，第 0 帧位置差不超过 20 mm，逐帧角度差的中位数小于 45°（results/submission/frame_refs_t2_mf4090.txt）。

前十行的证据是 results/submission/kit_facts.txt（stages/evaluate/submission_kit_facts.sh 对 9/28 旧 kit 8a169616… 的摘录，逐行带文件与行号）；jev results/submission/jev_claims_kit.txt，“打包器”“上传”两行在 results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0929.txt。最后一行出自 results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt，jev 在 results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt。9/30 读新 kit 源码，前十行说的做法都还在，打包器与 Track 2 README 的行号变了；逐行摘录还没按新 kit 重做。kit 本体放在 third_party/v2d_submission_kit/（新版在 update_a2de2aee/），不进 git。

为什么训练固定用逐帧（推断，jev 0.16，未核实）

帧号就是步数、计分按源帧对齐，所以只有逐帧回放（一步走一个源帧）能和计分格子一一对上；真实时长训练的 policy 一次评测跑 921 步（ep20），前 553 步会被拿去对完整的 553 帧参考，时间轴错位。这一条是读代码推出的，jev 没能从原文直接支持，还没在新评测器上实测。登记的 Day 2 分支两条都不完全吻合，由用户决定按“固定逐帧”处理（见 Day 2）。

9/30 的 kit 更新

9/30 重新下载的 kit sha256 是 a2de2aee…（服务器 Last-Modified 9/29 00:52 UTC-6，即 06:52 GMT），手上旧的是 8a169616…。逐文件比较只有三个文件不同：KIT_MANIFEST.json、tools/pack_submission.py、tracks/track_2/README.md（results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt）。顶层 README.md 没变，评测运行时仍指定 aee95f2。

Track 2 README 第 2 步新加的两句（原文）

“If the evaluator uses a different world frame, the packer converts its poses to the released frame after verifying one rigid transform across the episode. For these single-object episodes, the evaluator may label the object object.”

打包器源码里对应的改动（读新 kit 的 tools/pack_submission.py，行号按新版）：

  • 用第 1 帧的评测器参考位姿和发布参考位姿解出一个旋转加平移，套到整条 episode 的评测器参考上；逐帧残差都在 1 mm、1e-3 rad 以内（78–79 行），才把 rollout 的位姿换到发布的世界系（82–130 行）。超出就把这条 episode 记成问题，整次打包报错退出（350–352 行）。旧版在这种情况下只打印 WARNING，提示分数会和本地算的不一样、去问组委会（旧版 313–322 行）。
  • Track 2 的轨迹是 flash_chord_object_trajectories_v1 格式、却没有记录评测器参考位姿的，新版直接拒收（314–319 行）。
  • 单物体 episode 的物体名全是 object 时，不再因为和参考里的物体名对不上而报错（303–307 行）。
  • KIT_MANIFEST.json 里只有打包器和 Track 2 README 两个文件的 sha256 跟着变了。

对我们的影响（9/30 当时）：评测口径（1 个世界、逐帧、30 条全交、运行时 aee95f2）不变。aee95f2 评测器导出的轨迹是否用了不同的世界系、会不会触发换算，还没实测；出第一批轨迹后先核对里面有没有评测器参考位姿这一列。10/1 的新 kit 把运行时改成 8c778f9，换算的做法见上文“10/1 的新 kit”。接提交流程前按新 kit 重做 kit 事实摘录（旧摘录 results/submission/kit_facts.txt）。

评测运行时 aee95f2 的方盒支撑面问题

10/1 起评测运行时改为 8c778f9（见上文“10/1 的新 kit”），本节说的问题在评测侧已经不存在。保留本节，作为方盒三条重训的依据。

kit 指定的运行时 aee95f2（kit README.md:34）里，scene/support.py 与 v0.4.0 逐字节相同（md5 95e5e33f…），对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale（support.py:85–86, 114–116；results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt，jev 0.96）。30 条里只有 ep1（vacuum）、ep7（iron）、ep28（squeegee）用方盒支撑面（results/diverge/support_shapes.txt），在仿真里都被建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体，半尺寸 (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt，jev 0.97）。按这个几何，参考物体从第 0 帧起就埋在方块里：ep7 的 896 帧全部嵌入超过 5 mm，ep1 是 747 帧里的 519 帧，ep28 对两块方盒分别是 988 帧里的 608、651 帧（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt；jev 0.54，未核实）。本段三处 jev 都是第 5 轮，见 results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt。

上游 8c778f905c（#185，9/29 12:11 UTC-6）修了这个问题，比 aee95f2 晚 4 个提交（agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md 更正一节）。我们打了 #185 补丁的源码副本把 ep7 的桌面建成半尺寸 (0.42, 0.141, 0.005)（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt）。这份副本基于 v0.4.0，没有轨迹导出，只能用来训练和做对照。

ml40（L40S）上的对照 9/30 14:35 全部跑完，细节与判据见发散排查。ep7 冒烟 400 万步：v0.4.0 导入的 13 条（R0；fp16 种子 43–50 共 8 条；fp32 种子 43–46 共 4 条）没有一条学起来，8 条出 NaN，另外 5 条停在回合长度约 51、回报约 −3.7；#185 导入的 8 条（fp16 种子 43–50）都没发散，回合长度 243–309、回报 25.6–34.8。ep1 截到 6000 万步（单种子）：v0.4.0 fp16 从 30,001,152 步那份起 NaN，v0.4.0 fp32 从第一份（10,000,384 步）起就 NaN，#185 的 6 份全部有限。

按登记判据 A.3 的最终判定（9/30 14:35）：“v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是发散的触发”（H1′）得到支持，v0.4.0 fp16 发散 5 / 8 对 #185 fp16 0 / 8（配对种子 43–50），单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128 < 0.05；“fp16 存储是必要条件”（H4）被拒绝。结论的范围：H1′ 是在 ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）上检验的；ep1 只有上面的单种子旁证，方向相同；ep28 登记为只描述。fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少，这组对照说不了（results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt、results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final.txt、results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,3}_ml40.txt；jev 第 5 轮：最终判定 0.98、种子 47–50 0.98、L3 0.96、L0 与 L1 0.95，results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt）。

评测器遇到坏世界的处理在 aee95f2 与 v0.4.0 里相同：非有限世界只把位姿清零，只要有世界被判终止就报错（results/batch1/termination_facts.txt，jev 0.97，results/day2/jev_claims_batch1a_mf4090.txt）。第 1 批里 ep7 冒烟的 256 世界 rollout 有 172 个世界非有限、随后报错（results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt）；ep1 训练发散，3 份 checkpoint 的评测都报错（results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt）。官方只评 1 个世界，这个世界被判终止，这条就交不出轨迹。第 1 批里非有限世界都和这个报错一起出现，所以我们推测世界发散也会走到这一步，还没在 aee95f2 上实测。

我们的理解：排行榜不跑仿真，轨迹由我们自己用指定运行时导出，Kaggle 只对 CSV 计分（kit README.md:29–41）；带进 CSV 的 eval_code_sha256 只覆盖 evaluation/ 下的 __init__.py、harness.py、metrics.py、rollout.py（aee95f2 harness.py:123–135），#185 改的 scene/support.py 不在里面。所以换运行时要组委会书面同意，我们不能自己决定（计划 agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md 附录 A.6）。10/1 的新 kit 已把运行时改成 8c778f9，这一问不用再问。

原定要问组委会的三问（计划附录 A.6；10/1 的新 kit 发布后不再需要问，留作记录）
  • 能否用 aee95f2 + #185（或 ≥ 8c778f9 的运行时）训练并导出轨迹。
  • kit 指定的运行时版本会不会更新。
  • 如果不能换，这三条 episode（在 aee95f2 下物体埋在 1 m 实心立方体里）该怎么提交。

如果只能用 aee95f2，这三条评测时物体就埋在 1 m 实心立方体里；在这种几何上 ep7 的 13 条冒烟没有一条学起来，所以我们推测按 aee95f2 这三条交不出有意义的轨迹（推断，没在 aee95f2 上实测；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md 最终判定一节）。

如果允许换，我们的理解是 30 条都要在同一个运行时上重新导出：打包器注释写明 “one submission is one evaluator build”（新 kit tools/pack_submission.py:365–366，旧版 288–289 行）。#185 只改了 support.py 和它的测试（9/30 查的上游提交文件列表，没有落盘），被哈希的文件不变，所以我们理解打包器查不出混用，这一条靠我们自己守。ep1、ep7、ep28 怎么训等回复再定（执行记录更正一节）。

提交流程

  1. 每条 episode 选一个 checkpoint，用 kit 的 examples/run_policy_evaluation.py（在 8c778f9 运行时里，即 10/1 的新 kit 钉的版本；含 NaN 的 policy 不导出）评 1 个世界，得到 episode_XXXXXX.parquet。
  2. tools/pack_submission.py --track track_2_tier1 --dataset-root … --code-commit <GitHub commit URL>：核对帧与物体、必要时把位姿换算到发布的世界系、本地重算四项指标、带上各哈希，生成 CSV。用 10/1 的新 kit（0f2f9f4b…）；Tier 2 加 --frame-references，因为我们的参考是 v5.4，不是发布的带噪输入。
  3. submit/v2d_submit.py 校验 CSV 后传到 Kaggle 上这一档的四个指标比赛（同一个 CSV）；上传前要在 Kaggle 上接受这四个比赛的规则。每周 5 次，最后三天不限。

ep20 两条满配各按另一种回放补评一次：逐帧训练的改按真实时长评，chord_sr 0.79 → 0.03、MPPE 5.2 → 12.6 cm；真实时长训练的改按逐帧评，MP-SR 0.887 → 0.012、MPPE 3.9 → 18.6 cm（jev 0.74）。

撤回与改写的说法
  • “官方评测很可能用 1.67 倍速”：9/28 下午撤回（当时官方没写）；kit 发布后，计分按源帧逐帧对齐，训练相应固定用逐帧，但这是计分格子的结果，不是官方对“回放速度”的原话。
  • “评测若走 Isaac Lab，连仿真器都不同”：FlashCHORD 就是官方提供并推荐的路径，kit 的评测也是 FlashCHORD，撤回。
  • “两种训练换一种回放都崩了”（jev 0.32）：逐帧训练那条的 MP-SR 在匹配回放下本来就只有 0.0005，改为上面按指标逐项的说法。
  • 9/29 版“还缺什么”把 ep7 的评测器报错单列一项，写成训好的 policy 在 1 个世界里也可能发散：9/30 查到 ep1、ep7、ep28 的方盒支撑面在 v0.4.0 与 aee95f2 里都被建成 1 m 立方体，这一项并进上面的“方盒支撑面问题”。9/30 14:35 按登记判据判定 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发（H1′ 支持，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128，results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt），ep1 只有单种子旁证。

给组委会的邮件草稿第 2 版 agent-doc/organizer-email-draft-2026-10-01.md（10/1 16:30）删去了运行时与终止报错两问，空支撑面降成一句告知，剩 5 问：私有仓库的 commit URL 能否接受、代码能否在封榜后发布；Tier 3 的输入会不会发布；能否重复评测；Kaggle 队伍怎么登记；几条数据规则。用户 10/1 说先不急。9/28 的邮件与 Discord 草稿（agent-doc/organizer-{email,discord}-draft-2026-09-28.md）已被它取代。参赛说明会 10/7 12:00（UTC-6；官方写的是太平洋时间 11:00）。

Official evaluation and submission

The organizers released the submission kit at 19:46 on 9/28 (the modification time of the kit archive on the official site). It fixes the evaluation: each episode is scored from one checkpoint, one world and deterministic actions, the exported trajectory is scored frame by frame against the 30 fps source frames, and every submission must cover all 30 episodes. Training therefore uses frame-by-frame playback only (chosen by the user at 23:05 on 9/28). At 13:43 on 10/1 the organizers republished the kit and moved the evaluation runtime from aee95f2 to 8c778f9, which includes the box-support fix #185, so we settled the first three questions we had meant to ask them ourselves (next section). As of 07:00 on 10/2, Tier 1 has 27 evaluated frame-playback policies and Tier 2 batch 1 has finished 26 runs; the mf4090 half of the 8c778f9 compatibility check has passed and the 6 box runs are training; the submission flow plan v2 is reviewed and deployed, and the export chains on both machines wait for the box evaluations (see "What is still missing" below).

The 10/1 kit: evaluation runtime pinned to 8c778f9

The organizers merged upstream #188 at 13:40 on 10/1 and republished the kit at 13:43 (sha256 0f2f9f4b…). The README pins the FlashCHORD runtime to 8c778f905c, and the note on #188 says this version includes the Cube support scaling fix, that is #185 (results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt).

  • v0.4.0 to 8c778f9 is 6 commits (#180–#185). Under FlashCHORD, 11 files changed: evaluation code (trajectory export, provenance digests, two new metrics), scene/support.py and tests; no dependency or image file changed. On the training path only training/flash_sac/evaluation.py changed, by one import line and one field that defaults to None (results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt).
  • The kit's own flash_chord/scripts/evaluate_policy.py and its three Hydra configs are byte-identical to the same files in 8c778f9. The kit does not ship the runtime itself; we install a FlashCHORD that can export leaderboard trajectories.
  • How the new packer converts Track 2 poses: it solves one rigid transform from frame 0 that maps the evaluator's recorded reference onto the tier's released input, and converts only if every frame agrees within 1 mm and 1e-3 rad; otherwise the whole packing run fails (tools/pack_submission.py:78–79, 99, 118). When the input is not the released one, it has to be given with --frame-references, as npz files holding coordinate_frame=released_track2_world, frame_index [T] and pose_xyzw [T,1,7]. For all three tiers the packer computes local scores against the clean reference and prints them as a locally computed line.
  • The packer rejects the whole submission if one scored episode is missing and rejects worlds flagged environment_valid=false (from the code, pack_submission.py:274, 314, 399; jev 0.67, unverified), and it requires one evaluation-script digest and one evaluation-code digest across all trajectories. The sample submission has 22,989 rows, 30 episodes and world 0 only.

What we decided from this (decision record agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md, 9 of 9 claims verified with jev, 0.84–0.99):

  • All three tiers are evaluated and exported with 8c778f9; the runtime question is dropped.
  • The evaluator sets every ReconBody termination threshold to 1e6, so an ordinary task failure does not mark a world terminated; only a numerical blow-up does. None of the 96 recorded evaluations (393,216 worlds) raised that error. Two submission rules (adopted by the user on 10/1): a policy with NaN is never exported, and since 10/1 the training chain marks such runs NONFINITE and skips their evaluation; if an episode fails with "must disable task failure termination" at submission time, fall back to an earlier finite checkpoint of the same run, the only case in which an episode may use a checkpoint other than the final one.
  • The 16 floor-only sequences keep scene.support=false: 8c778f9 also fails on an empty support file, and turning supports off is the path it provides; evaluation rebuilds the scene from the checkpoint's config.
  • ep1, ep7 and ep28 are retrained once per tier on the #185 geometry.

Can checkpoints trained with v0.4.0 load in 8c778f9? The compatibility check on mf4090 passed this test from 06:33 to 06:43 on 10/2, with all 7 rows as expected (agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md). 8c778f9 exported 3 existing policies (t1-ep8 floor only, t1-ep6 with a cylinder support, t2-ep2 from Tier 2) on one world each, and t1-ep8 once more on 2 worlds; loading, structure and provenance all pass, with eval_code_sha256 and eval_script_sha256 equal to the 8c778f9 values. Under the two evaluators, t1-ep6 scores an ADD-AUC of 0.6335 and 0.6321 on 4096 worlds, 0.0014 apart, with a chord_sr |z| of 1.18, both within the registered limits. The guard stops ep1's NaN policy (exit 12); with the guard off, the evaluator fails and produces no export. One world against 4096: exported on one world, t1-ep6 has MP-SR 0, while the same checkpoint reaches MP-SR 0.57 on 4096 worlds. ml40 exports 2 more after Tier 2 segment C. Packing runs on the Mac with uv, since the server image lacks pandas and the kit is not on the servers.

Tier 2 packing needs --frame-references (generated)

The Tier 2 references are the v5.4 denoised trajectories, several centimetres away from the released noisy input, so packing without --frame-references is bound to fail with "do not differ by one rigid transform". The frame references are generated in the kit's npz format from runs/recovery/tier2_v54: v5.4 is stored in the OpenCV world frame with wxyz quaternions, the same convention as the released data, so only the quaternion order changes to xyzw. The packer also reads the clean reference for Tier 2 and prints local scores; our reading is that Tier 2 and Tier 3 local scores are never used to choose anything, and packing drops that line (an interpretation, not official wording, awaiting the user's confirmation). On 10/2 the 30 files were generated in this format (stages/evaluate/make_frame_references.py, which reads only Tier 2 files), and all pass the sanity checks: against the released noisy input, the frame-0 position differs by at most 20 mm and the median per-frame angle difference is under 45° (results/submission/frame_refs_t2_mf4090.txt).

What the kit fixes jev 0.80–0.99
