The last row comes from results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt, with jev in results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt. Reading the new kit's source on 9/30, everything the first ten rows describe is still there, but line numbers in the packer and the Track 2 README have moved; the line-by-line excerpt has not been redone for the new kit. The kit lives in third_party/v2d_submission_kit/ (the new one under update_a2de2aee/), outside git.

Why training uses frame-by-frame playback (inference, jev 0.16, unverified)

Frame number equals step and scoring is aligned to source frames, so only frame-by-frame playback (one source frame per step) lines up with the scoring grid one to one; a real-time-trained policy runs 921 steps on ep20, and its first 553 steps would be scored against the full 553-frame reference, out of step. This was inferred from the code; jev could not confirm it from the sources directly, and it has not been tested on the new evaluator. Neither registered Day 2 branch fitted exactly, and the user decided to treat it as "fixed frame-by-frame" (see Day 2).

The 9/30 kit update

The kit downloaded again on 9/30 has sha256 a2de2aee… (server Last-Modified 00:52 UTC-6 on 9/29, 06:52 GMT); the copy we had was 8a169616…. A file-by-file comparison finds three changed files: KIT_MANIFEST.json, tools/pack_submission.py and tracks/track_2/README.md (results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt). The top-level README.md is unchanged and still names aee95f2 as the evaluation runtime.

The two sentences added to step 2 of the Track 2 README (verbatim)

"If the evaluator uses a different world frame, the packer converts its poses to the released frame after verifying one rigid transform across the episode. For these single-object episodes, the evaluator may label the object object."

What changed in the packer source (read from the new kit's tools/pack_submission.py; line numbers refer to the new file):

  • It solves one rotation and offset from the first frame of the evaluator's reference pose and the released reference pose, applies it to the evaluator reference over the whole episode, and converts the rollout poses into the released frame (lines 82–130) only if every frame's residual is within 1 mm and 1e-3 rad (lines 78–79). Otherwise the episode is recorded as a problem and the whole packing run exits with an error (lines 350–352). In the same situation the old packer only printed a WARNING saying the score would not match local numbers and to raise it with the organizers (old lines 313–322).
  • A Track 2 trajectory in the flash_chord_object_trajectories_v1 format that does not record the evaluator's reference poses is now rejected (lines 314–319).
  • For a single-object episode, object names that are all object no longer fail the check against the reference's object names (lines 303–307).
  • In KIT_MANIFEST.json, only the sha256 entries of the packer and the Track 2 README changed.

What this meant for us on 9/30: the scoring rules (one world, frame by frame, all 30 episodes, runtime aee95f2) are unchanged. Whether trajectories exported by the aee95f2 evaluator use a different world frame, and so trigger the conversion, has not been tested; once the first trajectories exist, check that they carry the evaluator reference poses. The 10/1 kit moved the runtime to 8c778f9; how the conversion works is described in "The 10/1 kit" above. Redo the kit fact excerpt against the new kit before building the submission flow (the old excerpt is results/submission/kit_facts.txt).

Box supports in the aee95f2 evaluation runtime

Since 10/1 the evaluation runtime is 8c778f9 (see "The 10/1 kit" above), so the problem described here no longer exists on the evaluation side. The section stays as the reason for retraining the three box sequences.

In aee95f2, the runtime the kit names (kit README.md:34), scene/support.py is byte-identical to v0.4.0 (md5 95e5e33f…): for a USD Cube it reads size and ignores xformOp:scale (support.py:85–86, 114–116; results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt, jev 0.96). Only three of the 30 sequences use box supports, ep1 (vacuum), ep7 (iron) and ep28 (squeegee) (results/diverge/support_shapes.txt), and in simulation each box becomes a solid 1 m cube with half extents (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt, jev 0.97). With that geometry the reference object sits inside the cube from frame 0: in ep7 all 896 frames are embedded by more than 5 mm, in ep1 519 of 747 frames, and in ep28 608 and 651 of 988 frames for its two boxes (results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt; jev 0.54, unverified). All three jev scores in this paragraph are from round 5, in results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt.

Upstream 8c778f905c (#185, 12:11 UTC-6 on 9/29) fixed this, 4 commits after aee95f2 (agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md, correction section). Our #185-patched source copy builds the ep7 tabletop with half extents (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt). That copy is based on v0.4.0 and has no trajectory export, so it is only good for training and controls.

The controls on ml40 (L40S) all finished by 14:35 on 9/30; details and criteria are in Divergence investigation. In 4M-step ep7 smokes, none of the 13 runs with the v0.4.0 importer (R0, 8 fp16 runs with seeds 43–50 and 4 fp32 runs with seeds 43–46) learned anything: 8 produced NaN and the other 5 stalled at an episode length of about 51 with a return of about −3.7. The 8 runs with the #185 importer (fp16, seeds 43–50) did not diverge and reached episode lengths of 243–309 and returns of 25.6–34.8. With ep1 cut at 60M steps (one seed), v0.4.0 in fp16 is NaN from the 30,001,152-step checkpoint on, v0.4.0 in fp32 is NaN from its first checkpoint (10,000,384 steps), and all 6 #185 checkpoints are finite.

Final verdict under the registered criteria A.3 (14:35 on 9/30): the hypothesis that v0.4.0 building the boxes as 1 m cubes triggers the divergence (H1′) is supported, with v0.4.0 fp16 diverging in 5 of 8 runs against 0 of 8 for #185 fp16 (paired seeds 43–50), one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128 < 0.05. The hypothesis that fp16 storage is necessary (H4) is rejected. Scope: H1′ was tested on ep7 smokes (4M steps, L40S); ep1 has only the single-seed evidence above, pointing the same way; ep28 was registered as description only. These controls cannot say how much fp16 storage and the BN statistics each contribute (results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt, results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final.txt, results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,3}_ml40.txt; jev round 5: final verdict 0.98, seeds 47–50 0.98, L3 0.96, L0 and L1 0.95, results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_0930.txt).

aee95f2 and v0.4.0 treat bad worlds the same way: a non-finite world only has its poses zeroed, and any world flagged as terminated makes the evaluator raise an error (results/batch1/termination_facts.txt, jev 0.97, results/day2/jev_claims_batch1a_mf4090.txt). In batch 1, 172 of the 256 worlds in the ep7 smoke rollout went non-finite before the error (results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt), and ep1 diverged in training, with all three checkpoint evaluations raising the error (results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt). The official evaluation runs one world; if that world is flagged as terminated, the episode yields no trajectory. In batch 1 the non-finite worlds always came with this error, so we infer that a world that blows up ends the same way; this has not been tested on aee95f2.

Our reading: the leaderboard runs no simulation. We export the trajectories ourselves with the named runtime and Kaggle only scores the CSV (kit README.md:29–41). The eval_code_sha256 carried into the CSV covers only __init__.py, harness.py, metrics.py and rollout.py under evaluation/ (aee95f2 harness.py:123–135), and scene/support.py, the file #185 changes, is not among them. Switching runtimes therefore needs the organizers' written approval; it is not our call (plan agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md, appendix A.6). The 10/1 kit moved the runtime to 8c778f9, so this no longer needs asking.

The three questions we had meant to ask (plan appendix A.6; no longer needed after the 10/1 kit, kept for the record)
  • May we train and export trajectories with aee95f2 + #185 (or a runtime at or after 8c778f9)?
  • Will the runtime version named in the kit be updated?
  • If not, how should these three episodes be submitted, given that under aee95f2 the object is buried in a solid 1 m block?

If only aee95f2 is allowed, the object in these three episodes sits inside a solid 1 m block during evaluation, and none of the 13 ep7 smokes on that geometry learned anything, so we infer that these three cannot yield meaningful trajectories under aee95f2 (inference, not tested on aee95f2; execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md, final verdict section).

If a switch is allowed, our reading is that all 30 episodes must be re-exported on the same runtime: the packer's comment says "one submission is one evaluator build" (new kit tools/pack_submission.py:365–366, old lines 288–289). #185 changes only support.py and its test (from the upstream commit's file list, checked on 9/30 and not recorded in the repo), so the hashed files stay the same; as we read it, the packer cannot detect a mix and we have to enforce this ourselves. How to train ep1, ep7 and ep28 waits for the answer (execution record, correction section).

Submission flow

  1. Pick one checkpoint per episode and evaluate it with the kit's examples/run_policy_evaluation.py (inside 8c778f9, the runtime the 10/1 kit pins; a policy with NaN is never exported) on one world, producing episode_XXXXXX.parquet.
  2. tools/pack_submission.py --track track_2_tier1 --dataset-root … --code-commit <GitHub commit URL> checks frames and objects, converts poses into the released frame when needed, recomputes the four metrics locally, attaches the hashes and writes the CSV. Use the 10/1 kit (0f2f9f4b…); for Tier 2 add --frame-references, because our references are v5.4, not the released noisy input.
  3. submit/v2d_submit.py validates the CSV and uploads it to the tier's four metric competitions on Kaggle (one CSV); the rules of those four competitions must be accepted on Kaggle first. Five submissions per week, unlimited in the last three days.

How much a playback mismatch costs (Day 2 preflight, reference only)

Both full-recipe ep20 policies were also evaluated under the other playback: at real time the frame-trained policy's chord_sr goes 0.79 → 0.03 and MPPE 5.2 → 12.6 cm; frame by frame the real-time-trained policy's MP-SR goes 0.887 → 0.012 and MPPE 3.9 → 18.6 cm (jev 0.74).

Withdrawn and rewritten statements
  • "The official evaluation most likely uses 1.67×": withdrawn on the afternoon of 9/28 (nothing official then). Since the kit, scoring aligns to source frames frame by frame and training uses frame-by-frame playback accordingly, but that follows from the scoring grid; the organizers have not said anything about playback speed as such.
  • "If the evaluation runs on Isaac Lab, even the simulator differs": FlashCHORD is the officially provided and recommended path, and the kit's evaluation is FlashCHORD. Withdrawn.
  • "Both policies collapse under the other playback" (jev 0.32): the frame-trained policy's MP-SR was only 0.0005 under its own playback to begin with; replaced by the per-metric statement above.
  • The 9/29 version of "What is still missing" listed the ep7 evaluator error as its own item and warned that a trained policy might blow up its single official world too. On 9/30 we found that v0.4.0 and aee95f2 both build the box supports of ep1, ep7 and ep28 as 1 m cubes, so that item is folded into the box-support section above. At 14:35 on 9/30 the registered criteria found the 1 m cube to be the trigger of the ep7 smoke divergence (H1′ supported, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128, results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt); ep1 has only single-seed evidence.

Email draft v2 to the organizers, agent-doc/organizer-email-draft-2026-10-01.md (16:30 on 10/1), drops the runtime and termination questions and turns empty supports into a one-sentence note, leaving 5 questions: whether a commit URL in a private repository is acceptable and whether the code may be released after the freeze; whether a Tier 3 input will be released; whether evaluations may be repeated; how Kaggle teams should register; and a few data rules. The user said on 10/1 that it can wait. It replaces the 9/28 email and Discord drafts (agent-doc/organizer-{email,discord}-draft-2026-09-28.md). The participant webinar is on 10/7 at 12:00 UTC-6 (11:00 Pacific in the announcement).

Day 2

原问题是“官方回放未知时训练用哪种回放更稳”。跑到一半官方 kit 发布，计分按源帧逐帧对齐，用户选择按“固定逐帧”处理：取消剩下的真实时长臂，改跑新序列的逐帧满配。新序列的逐帧 policy 8 条里 2 条过 Day 1 判据，其余 6 条都卡在 MP-SR 为 0。

计划与判据登记于 67f1d86（9/28 16:30）；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day2.md（Day 2、Day 2b）与 2026-09-29-mf4090-night1.md（昨晚 mf4090 三条）。判定由脚本机械给出（stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py、day2_compare.py）。

jev（results/day2/jev_claims_results.txt）：ep26 逐帧通过 0.83、ep27 通过 0.89、ep13 与 ep10 不过且 MP-SR 为 0 0.87、ep10 / ep4 / ep2 不过 0.89、ep21 与 ep3 不过 0.82、Day 2b 与 night1 六条都有效 0.98。所有评测的步数都等于源帧数、没有非有限世界。

登记的判定

  • Q1（训练用哪种回放）：选 B 后两臂齐全的只有 ep26，有效计票序列不足 2 条，按规则不下结论（jev 0.97）；训练回放由官方计分格子定为逐帧。ep26 上按逐帧评，逐帧训练远好于真实时长训练（MP-SR 0.956 对 0.006）。
  • Q2（新序列上能不能过线）：逐帧格子 ml40 上 1/5 通过，连同 mf4090 昨晚 3 条是 2/8。登记规则是“两种训练都 ≤ 1/3 → 批量前先做 T1 与 T4”；真实时长一臂只剩 ep26 一格且通过，字面不触发。只看逐帧已经 ≤ 1/3，用户选择批量与 T1 / T4 诊断并行。
  • Q3（mf4090 的 400 万 buffer）：真实时长一臂两颗种子都不过，按规则不可用（jev 0.96）；第一次实测的种子噪声 gap −0.010、MPPE −2.2 cm。逐帧一臂只报数：ep20 上 400 万与 ml40 满配相近，ep27 是写这段时（9/29 早上）最好的一条，第 1 批按此让 mf4090 用 400 万分担。
  • Q4（只有地面的序列）：ep26、ep10、ep4 关掉支撑面后都建场景成功、正常训完。
两处执行偏差（都在对应结果出来之前登记）
  • mf4090 冒烟的显存上限：开链参数 23000 MiB 没写进计划，与登记的判据（只看 OOM 字样）不一致，三条冒烟被误判跳过；改成整卡总量后重核全过、重开（jev 0.78–0.97）。
  • 9/28 23:05 按 kit 改为固定逐帧：强制同步 ml40 改写来历，原训练链在 ep13 逐帧跑完后按设计停下，Day 2b 接着跑 ep10、ep4、ep2 的逐帧。

Day 2

The question was which training playback is safer when the official playback is unknown. Midway the official kit came out, scoring frame by frame against source frames, and the user chose to treat it as "fixed frame-by-frame": the remaining real-time arms were cancelled and replaced with frame-playback runs on new sequences. Of the eight new frame-playback policies, two pass the Day 1 criteria; the other six all stall at MP-SR 0.

Plan and criteria registered in 67f1d86 (16:30 on 9/28); execution records agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day2.md (Day 2, Day 2b) and 2026-09-29-mf4090-night1.md (last night's three mf4090 runs). Verdicts are produced mechanically by scripts (stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py, day2_compare.py).

What happened

Time (UTC-6)Event
9/28 16:33–17:20Preflight passed (identity check, ADD-AUC difference 0.003); all six ml40 smokes passed. The mf4090 smokes were wrongly failed by an unregistered memory limit, rechecked against the registered criterion and relaunched at 16:58 (execution deviation, marked as such)
9/28 19:46–22:30The official kit came out; checking it confirmed that scoring aligns frame by frame to source frames (see Official evaluation and submission)
9/28 23:05The user chose B: after ep13 frame finished, cancel the ep13 and ep10 real-time arms and run the next middle-tier (ep4) and long-tier (ep2) picks frame by frame; at 23:39 the original training chain stopped at its source check, as designed
9/29 00:24–07:45mf4090 ran three more frame-playback runs with a 4M buffer: ep21, ep27, ep3
9/29 08:27Everything evaluated: 12 Day 2 evaluations, 9 Day 2b and 9 on mf4090, all successful

jev (results/day2/jev_claims_results.txt): ep26 frame passes 0.83, ep27 passes 0.89, ep13 and ep10 fail with MP-SR 0 0.87, ep10 / ep4 / ep2 fail 0.89, ep21 and ep3 fail 0.82, all six Day 2b and night1 runs valid 0.98. Every evaluation ran the source frame count and had no non-finite world.

Registered verdicts

  • Q1 (which playback to train with): after choice B only ep26 has both arms, fewer than 2 valid voting sequences, so by the rule there is no conclusion (jev 0.97); the official scoring grid fixes frame playback instead. On ep26 under frame evaluation, frame training is far better than real-time training (MP-SR 0.956 against 0.006).
  • Q2 (do new sequences pass): 1 of 5 frame cells passes on ml40, 2 of 8 with last night's three mf4090 runs. The registered rule is "both trainings ≤ 1/3 → do T1 and T4 before the batch"; the real-time arm has a single cell (ep26), which passes, so literally it does not fire. Frame alone is already ≤ 1/3, and the user chose to run the batch and the T1 / T4 diagnosis in parallel.
  • Q3 (mf4090 with a 4M buffer): neither real-time seed passes, so it is not usable by the rule (jev 0.96); the first measured seed noise is gap −0.010 and MPPE −2.2 cm. The frame arm is reported only: on ep20 the 4M run is close to the ml40 full recipe, and ep27 was the best policy when this was written (morning of 9/29), so batch 1 let mf4090 share the work with a 4M buffer.
  • Q4 (floor-only sequences): ep26, ep10 and ep4 built their scenes and trained normally with the support surface off.
Two execution deviations (each logged before the results it could affect)
  • The mf4090 smoke memory limit: the launch argument of 23000 MiB was not in the plan and disagreed with the registered criterion (OOM messages only), so three smokes were wrongly skipped; with the card total as the limit all three passed and the chains restarted (jev 0.78–0.97).
  • At 23:05 on 9/28, the switch to fixed frame-by-frame after the kit: a forced sync of ml40 changed the recorded source, the original training chain stopped after ep13 frame as designed, and Day 2b then trained ep10, ep4 and ep2 frame by frame.

第 1 批批量

剩下 21 条 Tier 1 episode 的逐帧满配，两台分担、分 A、B 两段。9/29 09:29 开跑，9/30 12:35 前后最后一项评完。按 Day 1 判据通过 9 条、不通过 9 条、无效 3 条。无效的 ep1、ep7、ep28 都用方盒支撑面，v0.4.0 把这三条的支撑面都建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体，参考物体大部分帧埋在里面（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt 的脚本输出，jev 未核实）；它们的发散与这个几何的关系见发散排查。

判定与数字取自 results/day2/batch_verdict_ml40_batch1a.txt（通过 2、不通过 2、无效 1）、batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1a.txt（2、2、1）、batch_verdict_ml40_batch1b.txt（1、4、0）、batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt（4、1、1）。ep0 按世界算 MP-SR 0.438，但 ADD-AUC 低于不动基线，按判据不通过；ep5 的 MP-SR 只靠 5 个世界；ep11 判为“不通过（边缘）”，chord_sr 只靠 2 个世界。ep1 的评测报错摘录 results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt，NaN 扫描 results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt。

ep7：冒烟后评测器报错，桌面在 v0.4.0 里是 1 m 立方体

ep7（iron_round_table_02）的冒烟训练通过，之后 256 世界的诊断 rollout 报 172 个世界位姿非有限，接着官方评测器报错（harness.py:526；摘录 results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt），按登记跳过。现已查明：v0.4.0 的导入器对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale，把这条的桌面建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）；按这个几何，iron 896 帧全部埋在立方体里，第 0 帧深约 49 cm（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt；数字直接取自脚本输出，jev 未核实）。ep1、ep28 同样是方盒支撑面。排查经过与 GPU 对照见发散排查。

27 条放在一起看

本批有判定的 18 条，加上 Day 2、Day 2b 与 night1 的 9 条（ep2、ep3、ep4、ep10、ep13、ep20、ep21、ep26、ep27），都是逐帧满配、最终 checkpoint（results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt，脚本 stages/evaluate/batch_error_table.py）。下面是事后描述，不作检验。

5MP-SR ≥ 0.95：ep12 0.992、ep8 0.979、ep23 0.975、ep24 0.962、ep26 0.956
2MP-SR 0.5–0.95：ep22 0.764、ep6 0.562
6MP-SR 在 0 与 0.5 之间：ep0 0.438、ep27 0.321、ep29 0.174、ep18 0.152、ep20 0.016、ep5 0.001
14MP-SR 恰好为 0

SP-SR ≥ 0.7 的有 15 条。按秩相关（Spearman，n = 27）看各项序列特征与 MP-SR 的关系：路程 −0.69、总转角 −0.65、运动占比 −0.50、单段最大转角 −0.03、物体半径 +0.28。难的序列是“一共要走多远、转多少”大的那些，跟某一下转得多猛关系不大（我们的理解）。

失败原因（事后分析，推断为主）

门槛出自评测器源码（results/batch1/metric_threshold_facts.txt）：一个世界先对帧取平均误差，MP 要位置 < 3 cm 且朝向 < 30°，SP 要位置 ≤ 10 cm 且朝向 ≤ 0.5 rad（28.6°）。下面的“平均位置 / 平均朝向”是再对 4096 个世界取平均，看典型世界卡在哪一项。

  • ml40 B 段的四条不通过分三种（agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-batch1b-failure-analysis.md）。VOC 是给物体的虚拟辅助力，课程里从 1 分段降到 0，评测时没有。
    • 朝向跟不住：ep15、ep17 物体一直在手里（chord 0.982、0.831），但在 VOC 撤掉的过程中，训练时的物体朝向误差一路涨，ep15 从 0.11 到 0.53–0.55 rad，ep17 从 0.11 到 0.61–0.64 rad；最终平均朝向 30.4°、42.0°。
    • 有辅助也学不会：ep19 在 VOC 全开的第 0 段参考进度只有 0.32，最好到 0.61，撤掉后回到 0.54。
    • 辅助一撤就掉：ep25 的参考进度从第 1 段的 0.70 降到第 8 段的 0.22，物体终止比例从 25% 升到 78%；最终平均位置 114.0 cm，推测是掉了。
  • mf4090 B 段的 ep16（spray bottle，半径 0.16 m，路程 5.27 m，参考里只有左手接触，184 / 0 帧）：训练的 W&B 摘要没有 NaN，但最终平均回合长度 118.7、回报 15.25，同段另外四条是 374–552 与 68–133（results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt）。评测 chord 0、SP-SR 只有 1 个世界，平均位置 63.7 cm、平均朝向 85.4°，ADD-AUC 0.255 低于不动基线 0.266。推测训练阶段就没学起来，原因未查。
  • ep29、ep18 卡在 3 cm 的位置线：平均位置 3.5 cm、4.1 cm，平均朝向 5.8°、11.1°，都在 SP 门槛以内（SP-SR 0.989、0.966），MP-SR 只有 0.174、0.152。误差表把 ep22（3.7 cm）、ep6（3.3 cm）也标为卡在位置。ep29 的参考里双手都没有接触帧（0 / 0），序列名的意思是用左脚推 rolling luggage。
  • 分析文档按 22 条归纳：MP-SR ≥ 0.95 的 4 条（ep12、ep8、ep23、ep26）都是在地面上推或转的大件，解读里的共同点是用双手。新加的 ep24（watering can）MP-SR 0.962，参考里只有右手接触（0 / 519 帧），半径 0.19 m 落在文档里“小件”的 0.13–0.29 m 范围内，这条归纳要改。

分析文档列的改法（VOC 衰减更慢、加大朝向奖励、训练更长）都没试过，偏离官方配方，要另写计划登记；这些 Tier 1 结果也不能拿去调 Tier 2 / Tier 3 的方法。

每条的用时差别很大

ep1 第一个 1000 万步 4 分 53 秒（含编译），之后到 4000 万步每 1000 万步 3 分 17 秒到 3 分 20 秒，到 5000 万步那一段 6 分 22 秒，之后 7 分 17 秒到 7 分 25 秒直到训完；同一台上的 ep6、ep8 是 3 分 38 秒左右（results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a_2044.txt）。变慢的起点与 NaN 重合：第一份含 NaN 的 checkpoint 是 50,001,920 步（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt），本地 W&B 历史里 NaN 首次出现在 42,444,800 步（results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt），都落在第一段变慢的区间里。ml40 上的截断复现同样如此：L0（ep1、v0.4.0 导入器）第一份 NaN 在 30,001,152 步，每 1000 万步（不含第一段的编译，L1 同）从 3 分 18 秒变成 6 分 33 秒，之后 7 分 23 秒到 7 分 27 秒；L1（同样设置、换成 #185 的导入器）6 份 checkpoint 全部有限，一直是 3 分 9 秒到 3 分 10 秒（results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L0_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L1_ml40.txt）。这里只记重合，因果没查；之前写的“课程第一阶段之后接触计算变重”这条推测撤回。ml40 上 ep0 多用了约 20 分钟：9/29 10:32–11:46 同卡开着 3 个 viser 查看器、录了 3 条 rerun，这段每 1000 万步从 3 分 42 秒变成 5 分（results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt）。

节奏由 stages/policy/run_pace.sh 按 checkpoint 落盘时间算出，这个脚本训练期间也能在 Mac 上跑（经 stdin 传到训练机，不需要同步仓库）。

两条的参考动作对比（G1 运动学回放，2 倍速）：通过的 ep12 black_platform_turn_05 是在地面上转一个大件；彻底失败的 ep25 cyan_water_bottle_rotate_01 是手里连续转一个小瓶子。

Batch 1

Frame-playback full-recipe runs for the remaining 21 Tier 1 episodes, split across both machines in segments A and B. Started at 09:29 on 9/29; the last evaluation finished around 12:35 on 9/30. Under the Day 1 criteria 9 pass, 9 fail and 3 are invalid. The three invalid ones, ep1, ep7 and ep28, all use box support surfaces, and v0.4.0 builds all three as solid 1 m cubes with the reference object buried inside for most frames (script output in results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt, not verified by jev); how their divergence relates to this geometry is covered in Divergence investigation.

Plan agent-doc/plan-batch1-tier1-2026-09-29.md (independent review GO WITH FIXES, no blockers; registered in 6e7ae5c after the fixes); execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md. The queues come from stages/policy/make_batch_queue.py, and stages/policy/precheck_expect.sh ran the chain's assertions on all 42 expanded configs of both segments before launch (all passed). The verdict uses the final checkpoint only, and non_finite_worlds must be empty.

"Pass" here means the Day 1 criteria in stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py: the final checkpoint, evaluated with matching playback on 4096 worlds, has chord_sr > 0, maniptrans_sr (MP-SR) > 0, and ADD-AUC above the sequence's frame-playback do-nothing baseline. MP-SR and SP-SR in the tables are success fractions over 4096 worlds; the leaderboard evaluates one world per episode, see Official evaluation and submission.

Verdicts and numbers from results/day2/batch_verdict_ml40_batch1a.txt (2 pass, 2 fail, 1 invalid), batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1a.txt (2, 2, 1), batch_verdict_ml40_batch1b.txt (1, 4, 0) and batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt (4, 1, 1). ep0 has a per-world MP-SR of 0.438 but its ADD-AUC is below the do-nothing baseline, so it fails the criteria; ep5's MP-SR rests on 5 worlds; ep11 is "fail (marginal)", its chord_sr resting on 2 worlds. ep1's evaluation error excerpt is in results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt and its NaN scan in results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt.

ep7: evaluator error after the smoke; v0.4.0 builds its tabletop as a 1 m cube

ep7 (iron_round_table_02) passed its smoke training, but its 256-world diagnostic rollout reported 172 worlds with non-finite poses and the official evaluator then raised an error (harness.py:526; excerpt in results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt), so it was skipped as registered. We now know why the scene is wrong: v0.4.0's importer reads only size from a USD Cube and ignores xformOp:scale, so this episode's tabletop becomes a solid cube 1 m on a side (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt). With that geometry the iron is inside the cube in all 896 frames, about 49 cm deep at frame 0 (results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt; the numbers are taken directly from the script output, not verified by jev). ep1 and ep28 also use box supports. The investigation and the GPU controls are in Divergence investigation.

All 27 together

The 18 runs with a verdict from this batch plus 9 from Day 2, Day 2b and night1 (ep2, ep3, ep4, ep10, ep13, ep20, ep21, ep26, ep27), all frame-playback full recipe at the final checkpoint (results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt, script stages/evaluate/batch_error_table.py). What follows is a post-hoc description, not a test.

5MP-SR ≥ 0.95: ep12 0.992, ep8 0.979, ep23 0.975, ep24 0.962, ep26 0.956
2MP-SR 0.5–0.95: ep22 0.764, ep6 0.562
6MP-SR between 0 and 0.5: ep0 0.438, ep27 0.321, ep29 0.174, ep18 0.152, ep20 0.016, ep5 0.001
14MP-SR exactly 0

15 runs have SP-SR ≥ 0.7. Spearman rank correlations (n = 27) of sequence features with MP-SR: path length −0.69, total rotation −0.65, moving fraction −0.50, largest single rotation −0.03, object radius +0.28. The hard sequences are the ones that travel far and turn a lot in total; how sharply any single turn goes matters little (our reading).

Why runs fail (post-hoc, mostly inference)

The thresholds come from the evaluator source (results/batch1/metric_threshold_facts.txt): each world's errors are first averaged over frames; MP needs position < 3 cm and orientation < 30°, SP needs position ≤ 10 cm and orientation ≤ 0.5 rad (28.6°). "Mean position / orientation" below is additionally averaged over the 4096 worlds, to show where a typical world falls short.

  • The four ml40 segment B failures fall into three kinds (agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-batch1b-failure-analysis.md). VOC is a virtual assist force on the object; the curriculum lowers it from 1 to 0 in stages, and evaluation has none.
    • Orientation drifts: in ep15 and ep17 the object stays in hand (chord 0.982 and 0.831), but as VOC is removed the training orientation error keeps rising, from 0.11 to 0.53–0.55 rad for ep15 and from 0.11 to 0.61–0.64 rad for ep17; final mean orientation 30.4° and 42.0°.
    • Not learned even with assistance: ep19 reaches only 0.32 reference progress in stage 0 with full VOC, peaks at 0.61, and falls back to 0.54 once VOC is gone.
    • Drops as soon as assistance goes: ep25's reference progress falls from 0.70 in stage 1 to 0.22 in stage 8 while the object termination fraction rises from 25% to 78%; its final mean position error is 114.0 cm, which suggests the object was dropped.
  • ep16 in mf4090 segment B (spray bottle, radius 0.16 m, path 5.27 m, left-hand contact only in the reference, 184 / 0 frames): its W&B summary has no NaN, but the final mean episode length is 118.7 and return 15.25, against 374–552 and 68–133 for the other four runs in the segment (results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt). The evaluation has chord 0, SP-SR from a single world, mean position 63.7 cm and orientation 85.4°, and ADD-AUC 0.255 below the do-nothing 0.266. Our guess is that it never learned during training; the cause has not been looked into.
  • ep29 and ep18 stall at the 3 cm position line: mean position 3.5 cm and 4.1 cm, mean orientation 5.8° and 11.1°, both inside the SP thresholds (SP-SR 0.989 and 0.966), yet MP-SR only 0.174 and 0.152. The error table also marks ep22 (3.7 cm) and ep6 (3.3 cm) as held back by position. ep29's reference has no hand contact frames at all (0 / 0); the sequence name says the luggage is pushed with the left foot.
  • With 22 runs, the analysis generalized that the 4 runs with MP-SR ≥ 0.95 (ep12, ep8, ep23, ep26) are all large objects pushed or turned on the floor, and its reading named two-handed contact as the common factor. The newly added ep24 (watering can) reaches MP-SR 0.962 with right-hand contact only in the reference (0 / 519 frames), and its radius of 0.19 m falls in the 0.13–0.29 m range the document calls small, so that generalization needs revising.

The changes listed in the analysis (slower VOC decay, a larger orientation reward, longer training) are all untried and depart from the official recipe, so they need their own registered plan; these Tier 1 results must not be used to tune Tier 2 / Tier 3 methods.

Run times vary a lot by sequence

ep1's first 10M steps took 4 min 53 s (including compilation), then it ran at 3 min 17 s to 3 min 20 s per 10M steps up to 40M steps, took 6 min 22 s for the stretch ending at 50M, and then 7 min 17 s to 7 min 25 s until the end; ep6 and ep8 on the same machine ran at about 3 min 38 s (results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a_2044.txt). The slowdown starts where NaN appears: the first checkpoint with NaN is the one at 50,001,920 steps (results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt), and the local W&B history first shows NaN at 42,444,800 steps (results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt), both inside the first slow stretch. The truncated reproduction on ml40 shows the same: in L0 (ep1, v0.4.0 importer) the first NaN checkpoint is at 30,001,152 steps and the pace per 10M steps (leaving out the first stretch, which includes compilation; likewise for L1) goes from 3 min 18 s to 6 min 33 s, then 7 min 23 s to 7 min 27 s; in L1 (same settings with the #185 importer) all 6 checkpoints are finite and the pace stays at 3 min 9 s to 3 min 10 s (results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt, ckpt_nonfinite_L0_ml40.txt, ckpt_nonfinite_L1_ml40.txt). This records only that the two coincide; cause and effect have not been checked. The earlier guess that contact computation gets heavier after the first curriculum stage is withdrawn. On ml40, ep0 lost about 20 minutes: from 10:32 to 11:46 on 9/29 three viser viewers and three rerun recordings ran on the same card, and the pace went from 3 min 42 s to 5 min per 10M steps (results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt).

The pace comes from checkpoint write times via stages/policy/run_pace.sh, which also works from the Mac during training (the script is piped to the training machine, so no repository sync is needed).

The two reference motions side by side (G1 kinematic playback, 2× speed): ep12 black_platform_turn_05, which passes, turns a large object on the floor; ep25 cyan_water_bottle_rotate_01, which fails completely, spins a small bottle in the hand.

截至 2026-09-30 14:40（UTC-6）· 最终判定

ep1、ep7、ep28 发散排查

第 1 批里用方盒支撑面的三条 Tier 1 序列都训出了 NaN：ep1、ep28 在正式训练的 4000–5000 万步之间，ep7 在冒烟的约 241 万步，三条的批量判定都是无效。已经核实的是场景建错了：FlashCHORD v0.4.0 把这三条的方盒支撑面建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体。按这个几何用脚本重算，参考物体从第 0 帧起就埋在里面（脚本输出，jev 未核实，见文末）。上游 #185 已修，kit 指定的评测运行时 aee95f2 没有这个修复。ml40 上的 GPU 对照 14:35 全部跑完。按登记判据的最终判定：“1 m 立方体是发散的触发”（H1′）得到支持，ep7 冒烟的 8 个配对种子里 v0.4.0 发散 5 条、#185 发散 0 条，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128；H3（随机数值不稳）和 H4（fp16 存储是必要条件）被拒绝；ep1 的截断复现里，fp32 存储的 L3 也发散了。结论的范围：H1′ 是在 ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）上检验的，ep1 只有单种子旁证、方向相同，ep28 登记为只描述。

计划 agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md（CPU 部分第 2 版 af261bf；附录 A 的 GPU 对照第 3 版 10:35 独立审查 NO GO，第 4 版复审 GO WITH FIXES，按意见改成第 5 版后 11:07 登记于 1867ffd）；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md；结果在 results/diverge/ 与 results/ml40/diverge/。本页把三种说法分开写：“判定”是登记判据经 stages/policy/diag_verdict.py 给出的；“描述”是看到结果后的读数，不计入判据；推断都标成推断或未核实。

9/30 上午至 09:35CPU 排查 C1–C5（计划第 2 版）；08:57 起在 mf4090 看守 ep28
9/30 09:29:54ep28 连续两份 checkpoint 非有限，看守按登记规则 fc_stop
9/30 10:35附录 A 第 3 版审查 NO GO：v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体，实测确认；C5 按薄板算的方盒结论对 v0.4.0 的训练作废
9/30 11:08GPU 对照在 ml40 开跑（附录 A 第 5 版）
9/30 13:04种子 43–46 与 L0、L1 的中途判定落盘；13:06 起 ⑤ 排在 ④ 之后，等 ④ 结束自动开跑
9/30 13:23–14:06L2 13:23 跑完；④ 的最后一条 L3（ep1、v0.4.0、fp32 存储）接着跑，14:06 结束（results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt）
9/30 14:06–14:35⑤（R1、R3′ 种子 47–50，8 条冒烟）由 queue_after.sh 在 ④ 结束后 6 秒接上，14:35 结束，退出码 0；之后 ml40 空闲（执行记录“最终判定”一节）
9/30 14:35导入器最终核对（27 条全部有效）与按 A.3 的最终判定落盘（results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt）：H1′ 支持

现象

序列机器、阶段第一次出 NaN之后第 1 批判定
ep1 vacuum_left_lean_02mf4090，正式训练（9/29 09:54 开训）W&B 本地记录 42,444,800 步（课程第 1 段，voc 0.75）；第一份非有限 checkpoint 50,001,920 步之后每份都是 5892 个 NaN，训到 349,999,104 步；最后 3 个 checkpoint 各评 4096 世界，2445 / 2387 / 2342 个世界位姿非有限，评测器报 ValueError无效
ep7 iron_round_table_02ml40，冒烟W&B 2,406,400 步（课程第 0 段）最终 checkpoint 5892 个 NaN；诊断 rollout 172 / 256 个世界非有限，评测器报 evaluation must disable task failure termination before scoring the full reference（harness.py:526）；没进正式训练无效（没训）
ep28 squeegee_sweep_away_from_you_03mf4090，正式训练（9/30 09:01 开训）第一份非有限 checkpoint 50,001,920 步（40,001,536 步那份有限）60,002,304 步那份同样 5892 个 NaN，看守按“连续两份非有限”止损，不评无效

三条塌掉后的 checkpoint 都是 1,207,577 个参数里 5892 个 NaN。ep1 与 ep7 按张量扫过，非有限的张量完全相同，都在 state.0021–state.0030 这组统计量里（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_by_tensor_{ep1,ep7_smoke}.txt）；ep28 只数了总数。9/30 08:55 扫描时，其余 73 个已完成的 run（两台，含冒烟）最终 checkpoint 全部有限（ckpt_nonfinite_last_{mf4090,ml40}.txt）。

来源：results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_ep7_smoke.txt、ep28_nan_watch_log.txt、ep28_chain_and_record.txt；results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt、results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt；判定文件 results/day2/batch_verdict_{mf4090,ml40}_batch1{a,b}.txt。

ep1 变慢与出 NaN 在时间上重合，原先的推测撤回

ep1 在 mf4090 上前 4000 万步每 1000 万步 3:17–3:20（第一段 4:53 含编译），到 50,001,920 步那一段 6:22，之后 7:17–7:25（results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt）。ml40 上截断复现的 L0 也一样：3:18 之后，到第一份 NaN（30,001,152 步）那一段 6:33，之后 7:23–7:27；同一序列换 #185 导入的 L1 一直是 3:09–3:10（results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt）。目前只能说变慢与出 NaN 重合，原因没查。9/29 页面上“课程第一阶段之后接触计算变重”的推测撤回。

导入器把方盒建成 1 m 立方体（已核实）

v0.4.0 的 scene/support.py 对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale：85–86 行只取一个边长，114–116 行按这个边长建正方体（results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt）。三条序列的 USDA 都用 scale 标尺寸。用部署的导入器（md5 95e5e33f…）实际建一次，方盒的半尺寸全是 (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)，同一次检查里的圆柱对照正常（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）。

序列标注尺寸v0.4.0 建成#185 建成（半尺寸，m）v0.4.0 几何下物体嵌入 > 5 mm 的帧第 0 帧有符号距离
ep7 tabletop84 × 28.2 × 1 cm100 × 100 × 100 cm(0.42, 0.141, 0.005)896 / 896−490.4 mm（最深 −495.1）
ep1 chair_back2 × 12 × 27 cm100 × 100 × 100 cm(0.01, 0.06, 0.135)519 / 747−463.2 mm
ep28 两块 chair_back_top各 10 × 13 × 2 cm各 100 × 100 × 100 cm各 (0.05, 0.065, 0.01)608、651 / 988−476.5、−410.3 mm

标注尺寸取自 USDA（results/diverge/support_shapes.txt），#185 的尺寸取自 results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt。嵌入按物体网格抽样顶点到方盒的最小有符号距离算，负数表示在盒内（stages/retarget/support_penetration.py --cube-as-v040，results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt）。后两列是脚本输出；ep7 这一行的论断 jev 五轮在 0.39–0.65 之间（第 5 轮 0.54），未核实（见文末）。

  • 上游 8c778f905c（#185，“Honor authored scale for Cube support geometry”，9/29 12:11 UTC-6）改成按 size × scale 建盒。kit 指定的 aee95f2 比它早 4 个提交，support.py 与 v0.4.0 逐字节相同（md5 95e5e33f…），所以在官方运行时里这三条同样是 1 m 立方体。
  • 我们的补丁 third_party/patches/flash_chord-v0.4.0-cube-support-scale-8c778f9.patch 只取上游 support.py 的改动；stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh 生成打过补丁的源码副本（support.py md5 4d6eab11…，与上游 8c778f9 相同）。队列行写 V2D_FC_SRC=<副本> 时，scripts/dev/in-flash-chord 把副本挂到容器的 /workspace 并往 stderr 打一行挂载说明；写空的 V2D_FC_SRC= 就挂原来 third_party/video_to_data 里的上游 v0.4.0 源码（scripts/dev/in-flash-chord 第 9 行）。
  • 按 #185 的几何，参考本身的情况：ep7 iron_round_table_02 对 1 cm 桌面最多嵌入 0.5 mm；ep1 vacuum_left_lean_02 对椅背最多嵌入 4.1 mm；ep28 squeegee_sweep_away_from_you_03 在 239、209 帧里有顶点从两块 2 cm 板的另一侧穿出（results/diverge/support_penetration.txt）。ep1 另有 385 / 747 帧低于地面超过 5 mm（最深 23.8 mm），ep17、ep22 也穿地（123 / 440、136 / 719 帧），两条都训完没事（support_penetration_floor_controls.txt）。
  • 第 1 批里这三条的训练与评测都跑在 v0.4.0 的几何上，它们的无效判定说明不了 policy 在正确几何上能学成什么样。

同一条 ep7 iron_round_table_02 参考在两种几何下的样子（G1 运动学回放，2 倍速，不跑物理）：左边是 v0.4.0 的导入，桌面被建成 1 m 实心立方体，iron 从第 0 帧起就在里面；右边是 #185 的导入，桌面按标注尺寸建成薄板。

放大：一处出事，整条训练停下（读代码）

以下读自上游 v0.4.0 的代码，逐行带文件与行号落盘在 results/diverge/code_facts.txt（执行记录 C4）。

  • BatchNorm 的滑动均值与方差按 0.01 做指数平均，更新处没有非有限过滤（network.py:113–149）。一个含 NaN 的批次进来，统计量就一直是 NaN。
  • 梯度非有限时，_apply_adam_if_finite 只跳过参数与 Adam 动量（文档串原文 “Skip only parameters and Adam moments; callers retain all other attempted-update state”），同一步算出的 actor、critic、target 的 batch_stats 照写（learner.py:625, 643, 759, 776, 786）。
  • 每遇到一次非有限梯度，loss scale 乘 0.5（update.py:59, 76，配置 loss_scale_backoff_factor: 0.5）。W&B 本地记录里 ep7 冒烟的 loss scale 在一行之内从 262144 掉到 6.1e-05，ep1 从 4.19e6 掉到 2.1e-22，下一行是 0（results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt）。
  • G1 配方把回放缓冲里的观测存成 float16（g1_recon_body_flash_sac.yaml:76，训练默认 float32），计算用混合精度、compute_dtype: float16。

塌掉后的 5892 个 NaN 全在 state.0021–state.0030（成对的 256、1024 与 486 维统计量）。486 维的输入归一化统计量逐块看：位置、速度、位置差与目标历史整块 NaN（手在物体系、目标在物体系两块只有位置部分 NaN）；所有 6D 朝向块、只取自参考的未来根位置与参考相位都有限（results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map.txt）。

这部分能说明什么（推断）

代码解释了“一个批次出事，整条 run 就停下”：统计量变 NaN 以后所有前向输出都是 NaN，参数跳步护不住统计量。它没说明最先是什么出事。上午的推断是 fp16 存储把过大的位置、速度存成 inf；GPU 对照里 fp32 存储的 R2 照样发散，H4 被拒绝。R2 两条发散 run 的 NaN 下标与 ep1（fp16）逐位相同（results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map_R2_s4{3,4}.txt），按 A.3 归入“溢出发生在存储之外”一支，只描述。

GPU 对照（ml40 L40S，9/30 11:08 起）

按附录 A 第 5 版。每组只和它的对照差一个因素：R3′ 对 R1 只差导入器，R2 对 R1 只差观测存储精度，L1 对 L0 只差导入器，L3 对 L0 只差存储精度；配置逐键核对，意外差异 0（results/ml40/diverge/cfgcheck_diag_ml40_*.txt）。

组序列导入器观测存储种子长度队列

除 R0 外 buffer 都是 400 万；冒烟是 4,096,000 步。截断按配置做：总步数 60,002,304，学习率调度仍按全程 683,592 次更新。队列 ① 11:08–11:41（9 条）、② 11:41–11:57、③ 11:57–12:03、④ 12:03–14:06（L0 到 12:41，L1 到 13:02，L2 到 13:23，L3 到 14:06）、⑤ 14:06–14:35（8 条）。原来的 ep1、ep28 是在 mf4090 的 RTX 4090 上训的，L 组都在 L40S 上，所以 L 组的结论一律写成“L40S、单种子、与……一致”。

结果（全部 27 条）

“发散”按 A.3：冒烟看 W&B 摘要有无 NaN、最终 checkpoint 有无非有限参数；截断的 run 看任何一份 checkpoint。发散的冒烟最终 checkpoint 都是 5892 个 NaN。节奏的第一段含编译，不列。来源：results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_diag_smokes_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_ext4750_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,2,3}_ml40.txt、pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt。

按登记判据 A.3 的最终判定

判定文件 results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt（14:35，stages/policy/diag_verdict.py，输入是全部结果与导入器最终核对），执行记录末尾有“最终判定”一节。13:04 的中途判定（results/diverge/diag_verdict_seeds43_46.txt）在 4 个种子时给出 H1′ 的 p = 0.2143，按登记补跑 ⑤ 后再检验了一次；下表的判定栏以最终判定为准。

假设登记的检验读数判定
H3：与场景和精度无关的随机数值不稳R1 ≥ 2 / 4 发散 → 拒绝R1 5 / 8（种子 43–46 时 2 / 4 已拒绝）拒绝
H4：fp16 观测存储是“一处出事、整条训练死掉”的必要条件R2 ≥ 2 / 4 → 拒绝R2 2 / 4（种子 43–46；R1 对 R2 单侧 Fisher p = 0.7571；⑥ 没跑）拒绝
H1′：v0.4.0 的 1 m 立方体是三条序列发散的触发R1 对 R3′ 单侧 Fisher p < 0.05 → 支持；4 个种子时 p ≥ 0.05 → 补到 8 个再检验，仍 ≥ 0.05 → 不支持ep7 冒烟：R1 5 / 8 对 R3′ 0 / 8（配对种子 43–50），p = 0.0128；只检验了 ep7，范围见下支持
否定结论：仅修导入挡不住发散R3′ ≥ 2 / 4（或 ≥ 3 / 8）R3′ 0 / 8不成立
  • R0 发散，原 ep7 冒烟可以复现。
  • H1′ 的次要读数：R3′ 8 / 8 的回合 ≥ 64、回报 ≥ 2.37，回到非方盒冒烟的范围。
  • S：ep1 无 NaN、回合 73.0、回报 3.29，与非方盒冒烟一致（登记界限 ≥ 64、≥ 2.37）。ep28 登记为只描述、不判假设：按 #185 的几何，它的参考仍穿过两块薄板，分不开“修导入不够”与“参考穿板”。
  • L 组（L40S、单种子，只作旁证）：L0 发散、L1 不发散，与 H1′ 一致。L3 与 L0 只差存储精度，也发散，按 A.3 记为“fp32 单独不够”，与 H4 被拒绝同向。L2 登记为只描述。次要读数（第 1 段 35–60M 的平均回合长度，results/diverge/wandb_by_stage_L.txt）：L0 3.56、L1 204、L2 318、L3 3.55；登记的参照是 ml40 B 段 5 条第 1 段最短 209，塌掉的 run 约 5–6。
结论的范围

H1′ 是在 ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）上检验的；ep1 只有 L 组的单种子旁证；ep28 登记为只描述。能说的是“v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发，ep1 的单种子旁证同向”。说不了的是 fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少：H4 被拒绝只说明 fp16 存储不是必要条件。

描述（不计入判据）

v0.4.0 的 13 条 ep7 冒烟（R0、R1 8 条、R2 4 条）一条都没学起来：8 条 NaN；另外 5 条没出 NaN，最终回合都停在约 51、回报约 −3.7。#185 的 8 条回合 243–309、回报 25.6–34.8。原 ep7 冒烟（种子 42）在塌掉的前一行（2,355,200 步）回合也是约 50、回报 −4.11（wandb_local_history.txt）。登记的“发散”只数 NaN，那 5 条按判据算未发散，所以 H1′ 的 Fisher 检验比回合长度上的差别弱。回合停在约 51 的原因没查，把它归给 1 m 立方体只是推断。

冒烟基线也是同一个方向：31 条逐帧满配冒烟里，方盒三条的最终回合最短（ep7 5.0 且 NaN、ep1 53.0、ep28 57.4），其余 28 条都 ≥ 64.0（results/diverge/train_summary_table_{mf4090,ml40}.txt）；换 #185 后 ep1 73.0、ep28 87.0。

执行偏差：导入器核对看的文件

A.3 规定 #185 的 run 日志里必须有 in-flash-chord 的挂载行、v0.4.0 的必须没有，但写的文件是 runs/logs/<run>.s1.log，写错了：fc_run.sh:194 把 in-flash-chord 的输出写进 runs/logs/<run>.log，.s1.log 是 docker logs，只有容器里的输出，挂载行都是 0。判据的意思不变，改在 <run>.log 上核对（stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py，两份都数）。14:35 的最终核对覆盖五个队列，27 条全部有效，#185 的每条恰有 1 行指向补丁副本，v0.4.0 的每条 0 行（results/diverge/importer_gate_ml40_final.txt；13:02 的中途核对见 importer_gate_ml40.txt）。

对提交的影响

  • 官方每条 episode 只评 1 个世界，每次提交要有全部 30 条（见官方评测与提交）。那 1 个世界被判终止，评测器就报错，这条交不出轨迹；非有限的世界本身只被剔除并警告（aee95f2 rollout.py:429–432、harness.py:580–581，results/batch1/termination_facts.txt）。第 1 批里非有限世界都和这个报错一起出现，所以推测发散也会走到这一步，还没在 aee95f2 上实测。
  • kit 指定的运行时是 aee95f2，9/30 重新下载的 kit（sha256 a2de2aee…）仍写这个提交（kit README.md:34）。在 aee95f2 里，这三条的物体从第 0 帧起埋在 1 m 的实心方块里。
  • 推断（没在 aee95f2 上实测）：如果只能用 aee95f2，这三条评测时就是这种几何；v0.4.0 的 support.py 与 aee95f2 相同，在这种几何上 ep7 的 13 条冒烟没有一条学起来，所以推测按 aee95f2 交不出有意义的轨迹。
  • 评测摘要 eval_code_sha256 只覆盖 evaluation/{__init__,harness,metrics,rollout}.py（aee95f2 harness.py:123–135），#185 改的 scene/support.py 不在里面。我们的理解：换导入器不能自己定，要组委会书面许可。
  • 打包器要求所有轨迹的评测摘要一致（原文 “One submission is one evaluator build.”，旧 kit tools/pack_submission.py:286–300，新 kit 同一检查在 363–377 行）。我们的理解：若允许用 #185，30 条都要在同一个运行时上重新导出。
原定要问组委会的三问（计划附录 A.6；10/1 的新 kit 把运行时钉在 8c778f9 后不再需要问，留作记录）
  • 能否用 aee95f2 + #185（或 ≥ 8c778f9 的运行时）训练与导出轨迹；

As of 14:40 on 2026-09-30 (UTC-6) · final verdict

Divergence in ep1, ep7 and ep28

The three Tier 1 sequences in batch 1 that use box support surfaces all trained into NaN: ep1 and ep28 between 40M and 50M steps of their full runs, ep7 at about 2.41M steps of its smoke, and all three are invalid in the batch verdicts. What is verified is that the scene is built wrong: FlashCHORD v0.4.0 builds these box supports as solid 1 m cubes. Recomputed by script on that geometry, the reference object sits inside the cube from frame 0 (script output, not verified by jev; see the end of the page). Upstream #185 fixes this; aee95f2, the evaluation runtime the kit pins, does not have the fix. The GPU controls on ml40 all finished at 14:35, and the final verdict under the registered criteria supports H1′, that the 1 m cube triggers the divergence: over 8 paired seeds of the ep7 smoke, 5 diverged on v0.4.0 and 0 on #185, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128. H3 (random numerical instability) and H4 (fp16 storage is necessary) are rejected, and in the truncated ep1 reruns L3, with fp32 storage, diverged as well. The scope is narrow: H1′ was tested on ep7 smokes (4M steps, L40S); ep1 has only single-seed supporting evidence pointing the same way; ep28 was registered as description only.

Plan agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md (CPU part in version 2, af261bf; the GPU controls in appendix A got NO GO from the independent review of version 3 at 10:35 and GO WITH FIXES from the re-review of version 4, and were registered as version 5 in 1867ffd at 11:07 after the fixes); execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md; results in results/diverge/ and results/ml40/diverge/. This page keeps three kinds of statement apart: a verdict comes from the registered criteria via stages/policy/diag_verdict.py; a description is a reading taken after the results and does not count toward any criterion; inferences are marked as inferences or as unverified.

ep1's slowdown coincides with the NaN; the earlier guess is withdrawn

On mf4090, ep1 ran at 3:17–3:20 per 10M steps for its first 40M steps (the first interval, 4:53, includes compilation), took 6:22 for the interval ending at 50,001,920 steps and 7:17–7:25 after that (results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt). The truncated rerun L0 on ml40 did the same: 3:18, then 6:33 for the interval ending at its first NaN checkpoint (30,001,152 steps), then 7:23–7:27; L1, the same sequence with the #185 importer, stayed at 3:09–3:10 (results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt). All we can say is that the slowdown coincides with the NaN; the cause has not been looked at. The guess on the 9/29 page, that contact computation gets heavier after the first curriculum stage, is withdrawn.

The importer builds the boxes as 1 m cubes (verified)

For a USD Cube, scene/support.py in v0.4.0 reads size and ignores xformOp:scale: lines 85–86 take a single edge length and lines 114–116 build a cube from it (results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt). All three sequences give their box dimensions through scale. Running the deployed importer (md5 95e5e33f…) on them gives half extents of (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) for every box, while the cylinder control in the same check comes out right (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt).

SequenceAuthored sizeBuilt by v0.4.0Built with #185 (half extents, m)Frames with the object embedded > 5 mm, v0.4.0 geometrySigned distance at frame 0
ep7 tabletop84 × 28.2 × 1 cm100 × 100 × 100 cm(0.42, 0.141, 0.005)896 / 896−490.4 mm (deepest −495.1)
ep1 chair_back2 × 12 × 27 cm100 × 100 × 100 cm(0.01, 0.06, 0.135)519 / 747−463.2 mm
ep28, two chair_back_top boxes10 × 13 × 2 cm each100 × 100 × 100 cm each(0.05, 0.065, 0.01) each608, 651 / 988−476.5, −410.3 mm

Authored sizes from the USDA files (results/diverge/support_shapes.txt); #185 sizes from results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt. Embedding is the minimum signed distance from sampled object-mesh vertices to the box, negative inside (stages/retarget/support_penetration.py --cube-as-v040, results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt). The last two columns are script output; over five rounds jev gave the ep7 row's claim 0.39–0.65 (0.54 in round 5), so it is unverified (see the end of the page).

  • Upstream 8c778f905c (#185, "Honor authored scale for Cube support geometry", 12:11 UTC-6 on 9/29) builds the box from size × scale. The kit's aee95f2 is 4 commits older and its support.py is byte-identical to v0.4.0 (md5 95e5e33f…), so the official runtime also builds these three boxes as 1 m cubes.
  • Our patch third_party/patches/flash_chord-v0.4.0-cube-support-scale-8c778f9.patch takes only the upstream change to support.py; stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh builds a patched source copy (support.py md5 4d6eab11…, the same as upstream 8c778f9). A queue line with V2D_FC_SRC=<copy> makes scripts/dev/in-flash-chord mount the copy at /workspace in the container and print one mount line to stderr; an empty V2D_FC_SRC= mounts the original upstream v0.4.0 source from third_party/video_to_data (line 9 of scripts/dev/in-flash-chord).
  • Under the #185 geometry the references look different: the ep7 iron embeds at most 0.5 mm into the 1 cm tabletop; the ep1 vacuum at most 4.1 mm into the chair back; the ep28 squeegee has vertices coming out the far side of the two 2 cm boards in 239 and 209 frames (results/diverge/support_penetration.txt). ep1 is also more than 5 mm below the floor in 385 of 747 frames (deepest 23.8 mm), but ep17 and ep22 go below the floor too (123 of 440 and 136 of 719 frames) and both trained without trouble (support_penetration_floor_controls.txt).
  • Batch 1 trained and evaluated these three on the v0.4.0 geometry, so their invalid verdicts say nothing about what a policy could learn on the correct geometry.

The same ep7 iron_round_table_02 reference under the two geometries (G1 kinematic playback, 2× speed, no physics): on the left the v0.4.0 import, where the tabletop becomes a solid 1 m cube and the iron sits inside it from frame 0; on the right the #185 import, with the tabletop built at its authored size.

Amplification: one bad batch stops the whole run (from the code)

The following is read from upstream v0.4.0, with file and line numbers saved in results/diverge/code_facts.txt (execution record C4).

  • BatchNorm keeps its running mean and variance as an exponential average with rate 0.01, and the update has no non-finite filter (network.py:113–149). Once a batch with NaN goes in, the statistics stay NaN.
  • When gradients are non-finite, _apply_adam_if_finite skips only the parameters and Adam moments (its docstring: "Skip only parameters and Adam moments; callers retain all other attempted-update state"); the actor, critic and target batch_stats computed in the same step are written anyway (learner.py:625, 643, 759, 776, 786).
  • Every non-finite gradient multiplies the loss scale by 0.5 (update.py:59, 76; config loss_scale_backoff_factor: 0.5). In the local W&B history the ep7 smoke's loss scale falls from 262144 to 6.1e-05 within one row, and ep1's from 4.19e6 to 2.1e-22 and then 0 on the next row (results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt).
  • The G1 recipe stores replay observations as float16 (g1_recon_body_flash_sac.yaml:76; the training default is float32) and computes in mixed precision with compute_dtype: float16.

After the collapse all 5892 NaN sit in state.0021–state.0030 (paired 256-, 1024- and 486-dim statistics). Block by block, the 486-dim input normalization statistics have the position, velocity, position-delta and target-history blocks entirely NaN (in the hand-in-object and target-in-object blocks only the position part), while every 6D orientation block, the reference-only future root position and the reference phase stay finite (results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map.txt).

What this part does and does not show (inference)

The code explains why one bad batch ends the whole run: once the statistics are NaN every forward pass is NaN, and skipping the parameter update does not protect the statistics. It does not say what goes wrong first. This morning's guess was that fp16 storage turns oversized positions and velocities into inf. In the GPU controls, R2 with fp32 storage diverged all the same, so H4 is rejected; the NaN indices of R2's two diverged runs match ep1 (fp16) exactly (results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map_R2_s4{3,4}.txt), which A.3 puts in the "overflow happens outside storage" branch, as a description only.

The configs were compared key by key with 0 unexpected differences (results/ml40/diverge/cfgcheck_diag_ml40_*.txt).

Final verdict under the registered criteria (A.3)

Verdict file results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt (14:35, stages/policy/diag_verdict.py, fed all results and the final importer check); the execution record ends with a "final verdict" section. The interim verdict at 13:04 (results/diverge/diag_verdict_seeds43_46.txt) gave H1′ p = 0.2143 with 4 seeds, so as registered queue ⑤ was run and the test repeated once; the verdict column below is the final one.

HypothesisRegistered testReadingVerdict
H3: random numerical instability unrelated to scene and precisionR1 diverges in ≥ 2 / 4 → rejectR1 5 / 8 (already rejected at 2 / 4 with seeds 43–46)Rejected
H4: fp16 observation storage is necessary for one incident to kill the whole runR2 ≥ 2 / 4 → rejectR2 2 / 4 (seeds 43–46; R1 against R2, one-sided Fisher p = 0.7571; queue ⑥ not run)Rejected
H1′: the v0.4.0 1 m cube triggers the divergence in the three sequencesOne-sided Fisher test of R1 against R3′, p < 0.05 → supported; with 4 seeds and p ≥ 0.05 → extend to 8 seeds and test once more, still ≥ 0.05 → not supportedep7 smokes: R1 5 / 8 against R3′ 0 / 8 (paired seeds 43–50), p = 0.0128; only ep7 was tested, see the scope belowSupported
Negative conclusion: fixing the importer alone does not stop the divergenceR3′ ≥ 2 / 4 (or ≥ 3 / 8)R3′ 0 / 8Does not hold
  • R0 diverged, so the original ep7 smoke reproduces.
  • Secondary reading for H1′: all 8 R3′ smokes reach length ≥ 64 and return ≥ 2.37, back in the range of the non-box smokes.
  • S: ep1 has no NaN, length 73.0 and return 3.29, consistent with the non-box smokes (registered bounds ≥ 64 and ≥ 2.37). ep28 was registered as description only, with no hypothesis judged on it: under the #185 geometry its reference still passes through two thin boards, which cannot be told apart from "fixing the importer is not enough".
  • L group (L40S, single seed, supporting evidence only): L0 diverged and L1 did not, consistent with H1′. L3 differs from L0 only in storage precision and diverged too, which A.3 records as "fp32 alone is not enough", in the same direction as the rejection of H4. L2 was registered as description only. Secondary reading (mean episode length in curriculum stage 1, 35–60M, results/diverge/wandb_by_stage_L.txt): L0 3.56, L1 204, L2 318, L3 3.55; the registered reference is a shortest stage-1 length of 209 across the 5 ml40 segment B runs, and about 5–6 for collapsed runs.
Scope of the conclusion

H1′ was tested on ep7 smokes (4M steps, L40S); ep1 has only the L group's single-seed supporting evidence; ep28 was registered as description only. What we can say is that v0.4.0 building the box as a 1 m cube triggers the divergence of the ep7 smoke, and that the single-seed evidence on ep1 points the same way. What we cannot say is how much fp16 storage and the BN statistics each contribute: rejecting H4 only shows that fp16 storage is not necessary.

Description (not part of the criteria)

None of the 13 ep7 smokes on v0.4.0 (R0, 8 in R1, 4 in R2) learned anything: 8 went NaN, and the other 5 stayed free of NaN but ended at a mean episode length of about 51 and a return of about −3.7. The 8 on #185 reached lengths of 243–309 and returns of 25.6–34.8. The original ep7 smoke (seed 42) was also at a length of about 50 and a return of −4.11 on the row before it collapsed (2,355,200 steps; wandb_local_history.txt). The registered "diverged" counts NaN only, so those 5 runs count as not diverged, and the Fisher test on H1′ is weaker than the gap in episode length. Why they stall at about 51 has not been checked; blaming it on the 1 m cube is only an inference.

The smoke baselines point the same way: among the 31 full-recipe frame-playback smokes, the three box sequences have the shortest final episodes (ep7 5.0 with NaN, ep1 53.0, ep28 57.4) and the other 28 are all ≥ 64.0 (results/diverge/train_summary_table_{mf4090,ml40}.txt); with #185, ep1 reaches 73.0 and ep28 87.0.

Execution deviation: which log the importer check reads

A.3 requires the in-flash-chord mount line in the log of every #185 run and its absence for every v0.4.0 run, but names the wrong file, runs/logs/<run>.s1.log. fc_run.sh:194 writes the output of in-flash-chord into runs/logs/<run>.log; .s1.log is the docker logs output with only what runs inside the container, and has no mount line for any run. The criterion means the same thing, so the check reads <run>.log instead (stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py, which counts both files). The final check at 14:35 covers five queues and finds all 27 runs valid: each #185 run has exactly one line pointing at the patched copy and each v0.4.0 run has none (results/diverge/importer_gate_ml40_final.txt; the interim check at 13:02 is importer_gate_ml40.txt).

What this means for submission

  • The official evaluation runs each episode in a single world, and every submission needs all 30 episodes (see Official evaluation and submission). If that one world is terminated, the evaluator raises an error and the episode yields no trajectory; a non-finite world by itself is only excluded with a warning (aee95f2 rollout.py:429–432, harness.py:580–581; results/batch1/termination_facts.txt). In batch 1 the non-finite worlds always came with this error, so we guess a divergence ends the same way; this has not been tested on aee95f2.
  • The kit pins aee95f2, and the kit downloaded again on 9/30 (sha256 a2de2aee…) still names that commit (kit README.md:34). In aee95f2 the objects of these three episodes sit inside solid 1 m blocks from frame 0.
  • Inference (not tested on aee95f2): if only aee95f2 may be used, these three episodes are evaluated on that geometry. The support.py in v0.4.0 is the same as in aee95f2, and on that geometry none of the 13 ep7 smokes learned anything, so we expect that aee95f2 would not give meaningful trajectories for them.
  • The evaluation digest eval_code_sha256 covers only evaluation/{__init__,harness,metrics,rollout}.py (aee95f2 harness.py:123–135); scene/support.py, which #185 changes, is not part of it. Our reading: switching importers is not ours to decide and needs written permission from the organizers.
  • The packer requires the evaluator digests of all trajectories to agree ("One submission is one evaluator build.", old kit tools/pack_submission.py:286–300, lines 363–377 in the new kit). Our reading: if #185 is allowed, all 30 episodes have to be re-exported on the same runtime.
The three questions we had meant to ask (plan appendix A.6; no longer needed since the 10/1 kit pins the runtime to 8c778f9, kept for the record)
  • May we train and export trajectories with aee95f2 + #185 (or a runtime at 8c778f9 or later)?
  • Will the version pinned by the kit be updated?
  • If not, how should these three episodes be submitted, given that under aee95f2 their objects sit inside solid 1 m blocks?

为什么跑、怎么跑

  • 每次提交要全部 30 条，Tier 2 此前一条 policy 都没有。按我们对档位规则的解读，Tier 2 的 policy 只能从 Tier 2 的参考训出来（属解读；明文规则只有 Tier 1 那一条）。
  • 参考：runs/motion/tier2 的 30 个 parquet 两台 sha256 相同，物体位姿全部来自 runs/recovery/tier2_v54（results/t2/motion_tier2_sha_{mf4090,ml40}.txt）。支撑面只读 Tier 2 的拷贝：只有地面 16 条、圆柱 11 条、带 scale 的方盒 3 条，两台一致（results/t2/support_kinds_tier2_{mf4090,ml40}.txt）。
  • 配方：2048 世界、349,999,104 步、种子 42；ml40 用默认的 1000 万 buffer，mf4090 用 400 万；只有地面的加 scene.support=false；正式训练只留最新一份 state（V2D_KEEP_STATES=1）。
  • 分段：每台 A 段 5 条；A 段评完、且完成至少 3 条，chain_after.sh 才开 B 段。评测只评最终 checkpoint（V2D_EVAL_STEPS=349999104）。
  • 计划 agent-doc/plan-t2-batch-2026-10-01.md（第 2 版）。开训前代码与计划各由 Fable 5.1 独立审查一次，结论都是 GO WITH FIXES、没有 BLOCKER；两份共 6 条 MAJOR，全部改完才开链。其中最要紧的一条：冒烟刚训完就判，最新 checkpoint 写完不到 10 秒，非有限扫描不认它，会把正常的冒烟误判为失败。

执行偏差：ep10 改到 C 段

ml40 的 ep10 冒烟开跑约 2 分钟后，W&B 初始化时报 CommError: Error uploading run: context deadline exceeded，还没写任何 checkpoint（results/t2/ep10_smoke_failure_ml40.txt）。队列是 V2D_MAX_RETRY=0，链按登记的规矩把它的正式训练记 SKIPPED。冒烟目录里没有 state，也没有 DONE，链重跑时会从 0 开冒烟。01:17 在 ml40 上加了 C 段：期望表 stages/policy/queues/ml40_t2c.expect.tsv 只有 ep10 一行，队列行沿用 A 段的，等 B 段评完、完成至少 1 条就开。同样的网络超时可能再出现；出了就照这个办法放到后面的段补跑，不改正在跑的链。

判据（开链前登记）

  • 冒烟：沿用第 1 批的各项判据，另加两项：最新 policy 没有非有限参数；W&B 摘要里没有 nan，且 loss_scale 不为 0。rollout 帧数还要等于源帧数。不过的，正式训练记 SKIPPED。
  • 正式训练的结局分五种：完成、发散（看守止损，或训完但最新 policy 非有限）、失败、跳过、显存不足。都不人工续训。
  • 质量只报数：最终 checkpoint 按 Day 1 判据看（chord_sr > 0、MP-SR > 0、ADD-AUC 高于这条 Tier 2 参考的不动基线）。这些分数对照的是 Tier 2 v5.4 参考，不是官方计分用的真值：不和 Tier 1 比，不拿来调降噪器或超参。ep20 rice_cooker_clockwise_04 的参考里物体比桌面低 22.1 mm，它若不通过，先归到参考几何。

接下来（按实测用时估算）

ml40 的 B 段 9 份评测 07:00 开始，约 45 分钟；随后 C 段（ep10）冒烟、正式训练、评测，约 10:15 结束。每段评完先回流结果、跑判定、写执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-01-t2-batch.md，再过 jev。之后 ml40 跑 8c778f9 的兼容性验证，再接方盒 2 条（t2-ep1、t2-ep7）。两台的方盒评完后，提交导出链接着导出全部 episode（见官方评测与提交）。

27 Tier 2 frame-playback full-recipe runs: the 30 episodes minus the three box-support sequences ep1, ep7 and ep28. v0.4.0 and aee95f2 build those boxes as 1 m cubes; 8c778f9, the runtime the 10/1 kit pins, fixes this, and the three are retrained once per tier, see Next steps and to-dos. Why and how

  • Every submission needs all 30 episodes, and Tier 2 had no policy at all. Under our reading of the tier rules, a Tier 2 policy can only be trained from Tier 2 references (an interpretation; the only explicit rule is the Tier 1 clause).
  • References: the 30 parquet files under runs/motion/tier2 have the same sha256 on both machines, and every object pose comes from runs/recovery/tier2_v54 (results/t2/motion_tier2_sha_{mf4090,ml40}.txt). Supports are read only from the Tier 2 copies: floor only for 16, cylinders for 11 and scaled boxes for 3, the same on both machines (results/t2/support_kinds_tier2_{mf4090,ml40}.txt).
  • Recipe: 2048 worlds, 349,999,104 steps, seed 42; ml40 uses the default 10M buffer and mf4090 a 4M one; floor-only sequences get scene.support=false; full runs keep only the latest state (V2D_KEEP_STATES=1).
  • Segments: 5 runs per machine in segment A; chain_after.sh starts segment B only once A is evaluated and at least 3 of its runs completed. Only the final checkpoint is evaluated (V2D_EVAL_STEPS=349999104).
  • Plan: agent-doc/plan-t2-batch-2026-10-01.md (version 2). Before training, Fable 5.1 reviewed the code and the plan independently; both came back GO WITH FIXES with no BLOCKER, and all 6 MAJOR items across the two reviews were fixed before launch. The most important one: judging a smoke right after it finishes, when its latest checkpoint is less than 10 seconds old, makes the non-finite scan skip it and would fail a healthy smoke.

The claims themselves did not change. The 08:00 round on 10/1 is in results/site/jev_claims_sop_page_1001.txt.

Deviation: ep10 moved to segment C

About 2 minutes into ml40's ep10 smoke, W&B initialization failed with CommError: Error uploading run: context deadline exceeded, before any checkpoint was written (results/t2/ep10_smoke_failure_ml40.txt). The queue sets V2D_MAX_RETRY=0, so as registered the chain marked the full run SKIPPED. The smoke folder has no state and no DONE, so a rerun starts the smoke from zero. At 01:17 a segment C was added on ml40: its expectation table, stages/policy/queues/ml40_t2c.expect.tsv, holds only the ep10 row, the queue lines are segment A's, and it starts once segment B is evaluated with at least 1 run completed. The same network timeout may happen again; any run it hits goes into a later segment the same way, without touching the running chains.

Criteria (registered before launch)

  • Smokes: the batch 1 criteria plus two more: no non-finite parameters in the latest policy, and no nan in the W&B summary with a loss_scale other than 0. The rollout must also have as many frames as the source. A run whose smoke fails has its full run marked SKIPPED.
  • A full run ends in one of five ways: completed, diverged (stopped by the watcher, or finished with a non-finite latest policy), failed, skipped, out of memory. None is resumed by hand.
  • Quality is reported only: the final checkpoint under the Day 1 criteria (chord_sr > 0, MP-SR > 0, ADD-AUC above the do-nothing baseline of that Tier 2 reference). These scores are measured against the Tier 2 v5.4 references, not the ground truth the official scoring uses: they are not compared with Tier 1 and not used to tune the denoiser or hyperparameters. In the ep20 rice_cooker_clockwise_04 reference the object sits 22.1 mm below the tabletop; if it fails, the reference geometry is the first suspect.

Next (estimated from the measured run times)

ml40 started its 9 segment B evaluations at 07:00, about 45 minutes; then segment C (ep10) runs its smoke, full training and evaluation, ending around 10:15. After each segment is evaluated: pull the results, run the verdict, write the execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-01-t2-batch.md, then check it with jev. ml40 then runs the 8c778f9 compatibility check, followed by its 2 box runs (t2-ep1, t2-ep7). Once the box runs on a machine are evaluated, its submission export chain exports all of its episodes (see Official evaluation and submission).

当前架构

描述此刻代码实际的样子。每段之间只经文件交接，下游只读上游的产物，不 import 上游代码。代码在分支 track2/p1-training，已 push 到 GitHub。

  • 数据流从挑战数据到提交，每段用哪个脚本6 段
  • 模块仓库里六个目录各管什么、到了哪一步6 个目录
  • 运行环境Mac、两台训练机、两个镜像各自做什么2 台训练机
  • 依赖与缺口对外依赖的版本，以及还没补上的东西5 个缺口

Current architecture

What the code looks like right now. Stages hand off only through files: each stage reads the previous stage's outputs and never imports its code. The code is on branch track2/p1-training, pushed to GitHub.

  • PipelineFrom challenge data to submission, and the script for each stage6 stages
  • ModulesWhat each of the six folders owns and how far along it is6 folders
  • RuntimeWhat the Mac, the two training machines and the two images do2 machines
  • Dependencies and gapsExternal dependency versions and what is still missing5 gaps

数据流

三档走同一条链。实线框已跑通，虚线框未开始或受阻，绿框是训练所在环节。

挑战数据每档一个目录
→
Tier 1直接用
Tier 2v5.4 降噪 · 守卫
Tier 3视频重建 · 未开始
→
转换challenge_to_soma
→
重定向soma_to_g1 + 骨长
→
对齐检查间隙 < 20 mm
→
FlashCHORD 训练
→
评测
→
提交kit 已到 · 未接上
  1. 转换：challenge_to_soma.py 把挑战 parquet 转成官方重定向的输入目录。人体带逐帧骨长参数，丢掉会让关节整体偏 13.5 mm。
  2. Tier 2 降噪：export_tier2.py v5.4，进程一启动就禁止读取 Tier 1，每条序列产出一个位姿文件。
  3. 重定向与对齐检查：batch.sh 在一次性容器里跑官方 soma_to_g1，再查物体与地面、桌面的间隙。
  4. 训练：fc_train.sh，FlashCHORD G1 FlashSAC，全程 3.5 亿环境步；replay buffer 容量默认 1000 万，要 48 GB 显存。
  5. 评测：evaluate_policy.py 输出 ADD-AUC、MPPE 与成功率。评测 JSON 里的成功率字段 spider_sr_uncentered、maniptrans_sr、chord_sr 是 4096 个世界里的比例；kit 计分用的 spider_sr、maniptrans_sr 是 episode 的比例（SP-SR、MP-SR）。我们的评测以策略自己的参考轨迹为对照：Tier 1 的物体参考就是干净轨迹，Tier 2 的是降噪后的。
  6. 提交：kit 的 examples/run_policy_evaluation.py（8c778f9 运行时、1 个世界）出轨迹，tools/pack_submission.py 打包，submit/v2d_submit.py 传到 Kaggle 上这一档的四个比赛。每周 5 次，最后三天不限。见官方评测与提交。

Pipeline

All three tiers go through the same chain. Solid boxes run today, dashed boxes are not started or blocked, and the green box is where training happens.

Challenge dataone folder per tier
→
Tier 1used as is
Tier 2v5.4 denoise · guard
Tier 3video rebuild · not started
→
Convertchallenge_to_soma
→
Retargetsoma_to_g1 + bone length
→
Alignment checkgap < 20 mm
→
FlashCHORD training
→
Evaluation
→
Submissionkit in hand · not wired
  1. Convert: challenge_to_soma.py turns a challenge parquet into the input folder of the official retargeter. The body keeps its per-frame bone-length parameters; dropping them shifts every joint by 13.5 mm.
  2. Denoise Tier 2: export_tier2.py v5.4 blocks all Tier 1 reads as soon as the process starts, and writes one pose file per sequence.
  3. Retarget and check alignment: batch.sh runs the official soma_to_g1 in a throwaway container, then measures the gap between the object and the floor or tabletop.
  4. Train: fc_train.sh, FlashCHORD G1 FlashSAC, 350 million environment steps per full run. The default replay buffer size is 10 million, which needs a 48 GB GPU.
  5. Evaluate: evaluate_policy.py reports ADD-AUC, MPPE and success rates. The success-rate fields in the evaluation JSON are spider_sr_uncentered, maniptrans_sr and chord_sr, each a fraction of 4096 worlds; the kit scores spider_sr and maniptrans_sr as fractions of episodes (SP-SR, MP-SR). Our evaluation compares against the policy's own reference: for Tier 1 the object reference is the clean trajectory, for Tier 2 it is the denoised one.
  6. Submit: the kit's examples/run_policy_evaluation.py (8c778f9 runtime, one world) produces the trajectories, tools/pack_submission.py packs them, and submit/v2d_submit.py uploads to the tier's four Kaggle competitions. Up to 5 submissions per week, unlimited in the final three days. See Official evaluation and submission.

scripts/dev多主机同步与远端执行（V2D_ENV，训练链在跑时拒绝同步）、record（结果落盘带命令、md5、来历）、结果与 policy 回流、两个一次性容器入口（in-flash-chord 可用 V2D_FC_SRC 换挂源码副本）、镜像构建；9/30 加了 pipe-py（只用标准库的仓内脚本经 ssh stdin 在训练机上执行，不同步，链在跑时也能用）可用

运行环境

下文说"mf4090""ml40"指机器，"4090""L40S"只指显卡本身。

位置用途状态
Mac 工作树编辑代码与文档；scripts/dev/remote 同步到训练机后执行，V2D_ENV=ml40 切到 ml40。results/ 单向回流到 Mac：mf4090 回到 results/，其他主机回到 results/<主机>/可用
mf4090（实验室共用，9/28 起只有 rxzhou 在用；1× RTX 4090 24 GB，22 核）数据转换、降噪、重定向；Isaac Lab。FlashCHORD 放不下 1000 万 buffer；Day 1 的 100 万那条明显更差（两条同时换了 GPU 与机器，差别不能单独归因于 buffer）。400 万 buffer 跑逐帧满配可用（ep27 过线，ep20 与 ml40 满配相近），跑真实时长不可用（Day 2 Q3）；整卡读数 23.8 GB，至今没有 OOM。第 1 批分担一半 Tier 1：没发散的 6 条一条 1 h 57 min 到 2 h 14 min；ep1 从出 NaN 的那一段起变慢，用了 4 h 02 min（results/batch1/pace_mf4090_{a_2044,b}.txt）可用
ml40（实验室共用，9/28 起只有 rxzhou 在用；1× L40S 48 GB，空闲 45,458 MiB，28 核，111 GiB 内存）FlashCHORD 满配训练与评测；满配一条 2 h 03 min 到 2 h 31 min（第 1 批 8 条，随序列变，ep0 那条开着查看器；results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_{a_2044,b}.txt），峰值显存 40,273 MiB（Day 1）可用
镜像 robotic-groundingIsaac Lab 2.3.2 + 官方 toolkit：重定向与 Isaac Lab 训练（只在 mf4090）可用
镜像 flash_chord（22 GB）FlashCHORD：FlashSAC 训练与评测；两台都有。ml40 上的已随存储迁到 /lab_data，迁移后用 64 世界复核评测，ADD-AUC 与迁移前差 0.00015。FlashCHORD 源码默认从仓库的 third_party/video_to_data（v0.4.0）挂进容器；9/30 起 ml40 上另有一份打了上游 #185 补丁的源码副本，队列行写 V2D_FC_SRC=<副本> 时换挂它，只用于发散排查（见批量训练）可用

容器一律一次性启动、整仓挂 /repo，换机器要用相同布局，原因见 ADR-00003。

看 policy 效果：stages/evaluate/policy_viewer.py 包装官方 view_policy.py。viser 在服务器上开，经 ssh -L 转发到 Mac 的 localhost；rerun 录成 .rrd 拿回 Mac，用同版本（rerun-sdk 0.27.3）打开。在训练用的卡上开会拖慢训练，见特殊情况。

Runtime

Below, "mf4090" and "ml40" name the machines; "4090" and "L40S" refer only to the GPUs.

WhereUsed forState
Mac working treeEditing code and docs; scripts/dev/remote syncs to a training machine and runs there, V2D_ENV=ml40 targets ml40. results/ flows one way back to the Mac: mf4090 into results/, other hosts into results/<host>/Ready
mf4090 (lab machine, only rxzhou uses it since 9/28; 1× RTX 4090 24 GB, 22 cores)Conversion, denoising, retargeting; Isaac Lab. FlashCHORD cannot hold the 10M buffer; the Day 1 run with a 1M buffer was clearly worse (the two runs also differ in GPU and machine, so the gap is not attributable to the buffer alone). A 4M buffer works for full-recipe frame-playback runs (ep27 passes; ep20 is close to the ml40 full recipe) but not for real-time playback (Day 2 Q3); the whole card reads 23.8 GB, with no OOM so far. It took half of the Tier 1 work in batch 1: 1 h 57 min to 2 h 14 min for each of the 6 runs that did not diverge; ep1 slowed down from the interval where its NaN appeared and took 4 h 02 min (results/batch1/pace_mf4090_{a_2044,b}.txt)Ready
ml40 (lab machine, only rxzhou uses it since 9/28; 1× L40S 48 GB, 45,458 MiB free, 28 cores, 111 GiB RAM)Full-recipe FlashCHORD training and evaluation; one full run takes 2 h 03 min to 2 h 31 min depending on the sequence (8 runs in batch 1; the ep0 run had viewers open; results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_{a_2044,b}.txt), with a 40,273 MiB memory peak (Day 1)Ready
Image robotic-groundingIsaac Lab 2.3.2 plus the official toolkit: retargeting and Isaac Lab training (mf4090 only)Ready
Image flash_chord (22 GB)FlashCHORD: FlashSAC training and evaluation; on both machines. On ml40 it moved to /lab_data with the storage; a 64-world re-evaluation after the migration differs from the earlier one by 0.00015 ADD-AUC. The FlashCHORD source is mounted into the container from the repository's third_party/video_to_data (v0.4.0) by default; since 9/30 ml40 also has a source copy with the upstream #185 patch, mounted instead when a queue line sets V2D_FC_SRC=<copy>, used only for the divergence investigation (see Batch training)Ready

Containers are always throwaway and mount the whole repository at /repo, so every machine needs the same layout; ADR-00003 explains why.

To look at a policy: stages/evaluate/policy_viewer.py wraps the official view_policy.py. viser runs on the server and is forwarded to the Mac's localhost with ssh -L; rerun records an .rrd that is opened on the Mac with the same version (rerun-sdk 0.27.3). A viewer on a training card slows training; see Special cases.

已知缺口

  • 官方评测已随 kit 发布；10/1 的 kit 把运行时钉在 8c778f9（含 #185）。两台的 8c778f9 副本 10/1 晚已建、已核对；mf4090 的兼容性验证 10/2 06:43 通过，v0.4.0 训出的 checkpoint 能在里面加载、导出，ml40 排在 Tier 2 C 段之后。官方 1 个世界的分数与 4096 世界的均值可以差很多：t1-ep6 只导 1 个世界时 MP-SR 为 0，同一 checkpoint 4096 世界是 0.57。见官方评测与提交。
  • 选 checkpoint 的规则、批量导出（stages/evaluate/export_chain.sh）与 Mac 上的打包（stages/evaluate/pack_tier.sh）10/2 已写、已部署（提交流程计划第 2 版）；还没打出第一份 CSV。
  • runs/ 运行记录的字段已在批量训练列出，还没写进契约。
  • Tier 3 整条链路未开始。
  • 契约表全空；现有测试写明"暂无契约 ID"。
